		(function(vars){
			function isMobileShop(){
				return parseInt(vars.subShopId.slice(0,2)) >= 60
			}
			function getSiteName(){
				var siteName = vars.templateName.replace('ws_','').replace('tpl_','');

				if(['Content_data','Default','FullWidth','Landingpage'].includes(vars.templateName))
				{
					if ($('link[hreflang="en-GB"]').length) {
						siteName = new URL($('link[hreflang="en-GB"]').attr('href')).pathname;
					}
					else if ($('link[hreflang="de-DE"]').length) {
						siteName = new URL($('link[hreflang="de-DE"]').attr('href')).pathname;
					}
					else {
						siteName = new URL(document.location.href).pathname;
					}
				}
				return siteName;
			}

			vars.isMobileShop = isMobileShop();
			vars.isDesktopShop = !isMobileShop();

			//jquery existiert hier noch nicht, weil es erst spÃ¤ter eingebunden wird.
			// deshalb geht $(document).ready() nicht
			document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(event){
				vars.siteName = getSiteName();
			});

<script type="text/javascript">
		$(document).ready(function() {
	// Event-Handler

			// Dialog | Falls auf Loeschen geklickt wird
			$('.delivAddressManageSingleElementDelete, .cCardManageSingleElementDelete').on('click', function(e) {
				e.preventDefault();
				// Setzen der Siegel in den Hintergrund (Trusted-Shop)
				//ownLayer.putBackgroundSeal();
				// Anzeigen des Hintergrund-Overlays
				ownLayer.showOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
				// Bauen des Layers
				ownLayer.getSetOwnLayerDialogValues(this);
			});

			// Trigger fÃ¼r den ownLayer/ ownDialog
			$('body').on('click', '#ownDialog, .ownLayer, .opidOwnLayer, .opidOwnDialog,[data-ownlayer]', function(e) {
					e.preventDefault();
					// Setzen der Siegel in den Hintergrund (Trusted-Shop)
					//ownLayer.putBackgroundSeal();
					// Anzeigen des Hintergrund-Overlays
					ownLayer.showOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
					// Bauen des Layers
					ownLayer.getSetOwnLayerDialogValues(this);
			});

			// Galerie | PreflightUploader
            $('body').on('click', '.opidPreflightUploaderPreviewThumb, .orderOverviewOrderPreview', function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                // Vorladen der Bilder
                //ownLayer.preloadGalleryImages(this);
                ownLayer.showOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
                ownLayer.getSetOwnLayerDialogValues(this);
            });



			// Galerie | Falls auf die Preview geklickt wurde
			$('body').on('click', '#productHeadLeftPreview, #productPagePreviewtest, #productPagePreview, .productPageCBoxStd', function(e) {
				e.preventDefault();
				// Setzen der Siegel in den Hintergrund (Trusted-Shop)
				//ownLayer.putBackgroundSeal();
				// Anzeigen des Hintergrund-Overlays
				ownLayer.showOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
				// Bauen des Layers

				ownLayer.getSetOwnLayerDialogValues(this);
			});

			// Falls auf den Hintergrund oder auf das Schliessen-Symbol geklickt wird
			// Falls auf den Abbrechen-Button geklickt wurde
			$('body').on('click', '.opidOwnLayerBackgroundOverlayRemove, #ownLayerHeaderRight, #ownDialogBtnCancel, #ownDialogBtnClose, #customOwnDialogBtnCancel, #geoIPCancelBtn, #ownDialogBtnToProduct, #ownDialogBtnReady, .opidCloseOwnDialog, .opidOwnDialogBtnCancel ', function() {
				if(!$('.opidOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay').hasClass('opidOwnLayerBackgroundOverlayRemove'))  {
                    // Ermoeglicht das Schliessen des Dialogs ueber eine Klick auf den Overlay wieder
                    ownLayer.overlayEnableCloseLayerDialog();
                }
                if($('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').hasClass('opidOwnLayerCancelOrderWrapper')) {
                    orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.resetPaymentFlowsAndSteps();
                    orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.resetCancelationValues();
                    orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.resetIsComment();
                    orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.resetErrorFormating();
                } else if($('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').hasClass('ownLayerOrderOverviewPreview')) {
                    orderOverviewObj.resetOrderOveweviewOwnDialog();
                }
			    ownLayer.removeShortLinkOwnLayer();
				// Setzen der Siegel in den Vordergrund (Trusted-Shop)
				//ownLayer.putFrontSeal();
				// Ausblenden des Hintergrund-Overlays
				ownLayer.hideOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
				// Entfernt die Kennzeichnungsklasse eines "Galerie-Layer"
				ownLayer.removeClassForGallery();
				// Ausblenden des Layers
				ownLayer.hideOwnLayer();
				// Ausblenden der Galerie-Navigation
				ownLayer.hideOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
				// Ausblenden der Bilderzusatzinformation
				ownLayer.hideOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo();
				// Setzen des Data-Attributes auf das aktuelle Bild
				ownLayer.setDataHovered();
                ownLayer.setDataHoveredJSON();
				// entfernt das iFrame um das Video zu stoppen
				ownLayer.removeIframeVideo();
				// Aktualisiert Trusted Shop
				
			});

			// Falls die ESC-Taste gedrÃ¼ckt wurde
			$(document).keyup(function(e) {
				if (e.keyCode == 27) {
					ownLayer.removeShortLinkOwnLayer();
					// Setzen der Siegel in den Vordergrund (Trusted-Shop)
					//ownLayer.putFrontSeal();
					// Ausblenden des Hintergrund-Overlays
					ownLayer.hideOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
					// Entfernt die Kennzeichnungsklasse eines "Galerie-Layer"
					ownLayer.removeClassForGallery();
					// Ausblenden des Layers
					ownLayer.hideOwnLayer();
					// Ausblenden der Galerie-Navigation
					ownLayer.hideOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
					// Ausblenden der Bilderzusatzinformation
					ownLayer.hideOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo();
					// Setzen des Data-Attributes auf das aktuelle Bild
					ownLayer.setDataHovered();
					ownLayer.setDataHoveredJSON();
					// entfernt das iFrame um das Video zu stoppen
					ownLayer.removeIframeVideo();
					// Aktualisiert Trusted Shop
					
				}
			});

            // Galerie - Preflight | Falls in das Input fuer die Seitenzahl geklickt wird

                ownLayer.restrictImageNumberInput(e);

            // Galerie | Falls auf den Weiter Button oder den Weiter Handler geklickt wurde
			$('#ownLayerGalleryForward, #ownLayerBodyRight').on('click', function() {
                if($(this).hasClass('ownLayerPreflightGalleryForward') || $(this).hasClass('ownLayerPreflightBodyRight')) {

                    ownLayer.showNextPreflightGalleryElementLayer();
                    // Preloader einblenden
                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').append(oplib.preloader('opidOwnLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader','ownLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader'));
                    } else {

                    ownLayer.showNextGalleryElementLayer();
                    ownLayer.showNextGalleryElementLayerJSON();
                }
			});

			// Galerie | Falls auf den Zurueck Button oder den Zurueck Handler geklickt wurde
			$('#ownLayerGalleryBack, #ownLayerBodyLeft').on('click', function() {
                if($(this).hasClass('ownLayerPreflightGalleryBack') || $(this).hasClass('ownLayerPreflightBodyLeft')) {

                    ownLayer.showPreviousPreflightGalleryElementLayer();
                    // Preloader einblenden
                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').append(oplib.preloader('opidOwnLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader','ownLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader'));
                    } else {

                    ownLayer.showPreviousGalleryElementLayer();
                    ownLayer.showPreviousGalleryElementLayerJSON();
                }
			});

			// Galerie | Falls auf den Pfeil nach Links oder Rechts geklickt wurde
			$('html').keydown(function(e){
			    var element = e.target,
			        uploadpages = $('.opidOwnLayerBody').find('img').attr('uploadpages');
			    if ($('.ownLayerWrapperGallery').is(':visible')) {
				    // Das Blaettern ueber die Pfeile ist nur moeglich, wenn man sich nicht in dem Input fuer die Seitenzahl befindet
                    if(!$(element).hasClass('opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent')) {
                        if (e.keyCode == 37) {
                            if($('#ownLayerWrapper').hasClass('ownLayerPreflightWrapperGallery')) {
                                if(uploadpages >= 2) {
    
                                    ownLayer.showPreviousPreflightGalleryElementLayer();
                                    // Preloader einblenden
                                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').append(oplib.preloader('opidOwnLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader','ownLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader'));
                                }
                            } else {
    
                                ownLayer.showPreviousGalleryElementLayer();
                                ownLayer.showPreviousGalleryElementLayerJSON();
                            }
                        } else if (e.keyCode == 39) {
                            if($('#ownLayerWrapper').hasClass('ownLayerPreflightWrapperGallery')) {
                                if(uploadpages >= 2) {
    
                                    ownLayer.showNextPreflightGalleryElementLayer();
                                    // Preloader einblenden
                                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').append(oplib.preloader('opidOwnLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader','ownLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader'));
                                }
                            } else {
    
                                ownLayer.showNextGalleryElementLayer();
                                ownLayer.showNextGalleryElementLayerJSON();
                            }
                        }
                    }
				}
			});

			// Falls sich die Fenstergroesse aendert
			$(window).resize(function() {
				// Neues zentrieren des Layers
				//ownLayer.centerOwnLayer();
				ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');

				// Darf nur fÃ¼r den normalen Layer ausgefÃ¼hrt werden (Nicht Galerie und Dialog)
				ownLayer.setContentHeight();
			});
		});

			buildOwnDialogType: function(element, elementData) {
//console.log('element = ',element);
				var ownDialogType = elementData.ownDialogType,
					targetUrl,
					targetUrlOne,
					targetUrlTwo,
					targetUrlThree,
					targetContent;
				// type: delete | memolist | download | memolist | default | basket
				switch (ownDialogType) {
					case "basket" :
						targetUrl = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch/websale8/?Ctx=%257bver%252f8%252fver%257d%257bst%252f3ee%252fst%257d%257bcmd%252f0%252fcmd%257d%257bm%252fwebsale%252fm%257d%257bs%252fdiedruckerei%252fs%257d%257bl%252f15%252daa%252fl%257d%257bmd5%252f02422c38fd975ab3edbe46f8c3bc1c74%252fmd5%257d';
						$('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnToBasket', function() {
							window.location.href = targetUrl;
						});
						break;
                    case "cancelOrder" :
                        var orderId = elementData.ownDialogOrderId;
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnNext');
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnBack');
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnYes');
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnNo');
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk');
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnCancelOrder');
                        $('body').off('change', 'input[name="orderIsPaid"]');
                        $('body').off('change', 'input[name="orderIsRefund"]');

                        $('body').on('change', 'input[name="orderIsPaid"]', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkIsPaid(this, orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('change', 'input[name="orderIsRefund"]', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkIsRefund(this, orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnNext', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.step++;
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkBuildDialogs('btnNext', orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnYes', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.step++;
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkBuildDialogs('btnYes', orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnNo', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.step++;
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkBuildDialogs('btnNo', orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnBack', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.step--;
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.checkBuildDialogs('btnBack', orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnCancelOrder', function() {
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.startCancelOrder(orderId);
                        });
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk', function() {
                            ownLayer.hideOwnLayer();
                            ownLayer.hideOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay();
                            orderOverviewCancelOrderObj.cancelOrderFinalTemplate(orderId);
                        });
                        break;
					case "default" :
						break;
					case "delete" :
						targetUrl = $(element).attr('href');

						$('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk', function() {
							window.location.href = targetUrl;
						});
						break;
                    case "deleteBasket" :
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk');
                        targetUrl = $(element).attr('href');
                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk', function() {
                            // Aufruf der Funktion fÃ¼r den Ajax-deleteBasket
                            basketObj.ajaxDeleteProductBasket(targetUrl, element);
                        });
                        break;
                    case "deleteUpload" :
                        $('body').off('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk');
                        targetUrl = $(element).attr('href');
                        $('body').one('click', '#ownDialogBtnOk', function() {
                            preflightUploaderObj.preflightUploaderDeleteUpload(targetUrl);
                        });
                        break;

						});
						break;
					/*case "loadTemplate" :
						targetUrl = $(element).attr('href');
						break; */

						targetUrlTwo = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch/websale8/?Ctx=%257bver%252f8%252fver%257d%257bst%252f407%252fst%257d%257bcmd%252f1%252fcmd%257d%257bm%252fwebsale%252fm%257d%257bs%252fdiedruckerei%252fs%257d%257bl%252f15%252daa%252fl%257d%257bssl%252fx%252fssl%257d%257bmd5%252f4f45d4a85ad0b5216ae9417ce021f982%252fmd5%257d&type=LoginUser&otp3=w2p-request';
						targetUrlThree = $(element).attr('href');
						$('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnNoLogin', function() {
							window.location.href = targetUrlOne;

                    case "web2printmulticart" :

                        targetUrlOne = basketW2PCreateLink;

                        targetUrlThree = $(element).attr('href');

                        $('body').on('click', '#ownDialogBtnLogin', function() {

                        });
                        break;
					case "web2printBasket" :

						   da der Submit-Button der Original-Form benÃ¶tigt wird. */

                    case "reorderDiscardNewConfig" :

			buildOwnGalleryLayer: function(imageDimensionsArr) {
				$('.ownLayerBodyWrapper').css({
					'min-width'     : '570px',
					'width'         : imageDimensionsArr[0],
					'height'        : imageDimensionsArr[1]

				}).addClass('globalTextAlignCenter');

				$('.ownLayerWrapper').css({
					'min-width'     : '600px',
					'width'         : imageDimensionsArr[0]
				});

				//$('.ownLayerBodyLeft, .ownLayerBodyRight').show();
			},
			buildOwnLayer: function(elementData) {
				$('.ownLayerWrapper').css({
					'width'         : elementData.ownLayerWidth,
					'height'        : elementData.ownLayerHeight
				});

				$('.ownLayerHeaderWrapper').addClass('ownLayerHeaderFooterBackground');
				$('.ownLayerFooterWrapper').hide();
				$('#ownLayerBody').css({'height':'100%'});
				$('.ownLayerWrapper').data('ownlayer','ownlayertrue');
			},
			calculateOwnLayerHeader: function() {
				var ownLayerWidth   = $('.ownLayerHeader').width(),
					ownLayerHeaderLeftWidth = ownLayerWidth - (parseInt($('.ownLayerHeaderRight').outerWidth()));
				$('.ownLayerHeaderLeft').css('width', ownLayerHeaderLeftWidth);
			},
			centerElement: function(element) {

				var windowWidth  = $(window).width(),
					windowHeight = $(window).height(),
					windowScrollTop = $(window).scrollTop(),
					elementWidth = $('#'+element).outerWidth(),
					elementHeight = $('#'+element).outerHeight(),
					elementLeft,
					elementTop;
				elementLeft = parseInt((windowWidth-elementWidth)/2);
				elementTop = parseInt((windowHeight-elementHeight)/2);
				$('.'+element).css({
					'left'          : elementLeft+'px',
					'top'           : elementTop+'px'
				});		 
			},
			centerLayerPreloader: function() {
				var ownLayerBodyWrapperWidth = $('#ownLayerBodyWrapper').width(),
					ownLayerBodyWrapperHeight = $('#ownLayerBodyWrapper').height(),			
					ownLayerPreloaderWidth = $('.ownLayerPreloader .sk-cube-grid').width(),
					ownLayerPreloaderHeight = $('.ownLayerPreloader .sk-cube-grid').height(),				
					newLeft  = parseInt((ownLayerBodyWrapperWidth-ownLayerPreloaderWidth))/2,
					newTop = parseInt((ownLayerBodyWrapperHeight-ownLayerPreloaderHeight))/2;
				$('.ownLayerPreloader .sk-cube-grid').css({
				  'margin-left': newLeft,
				  'margin-top' : newTop
				});	
			},
            checkValidateImageNumberInput: function(e) {
                var checkValue = parseInt($('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').val()),
                    quantityElements = parseInt($('#ownLayerBody img').attr('uploadpages')),
                    currentImageSrc = $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src'),
                    currentImageSrcParts = currentImageSrc.split('&');

                if(e.keyCode === 16) {
                    ownLayer.pressedShift = false;
                };
                if(e.keyCode === 17) {
                    ownLayer.pressedStrg = false;
                };
                if(e.keyCode === 18) {
                    ownLayer.pressedAlt = false;
                };

                // Falls die Zahl 0 ist oder die Zahl groesser als die maximale Seitenzahl
                if(checkValue === 0 || checkValue > quantityElements) {
                    ownLayer.removeLastCharacter();
                }

                if(e.keyCode === 13) {
                    checkValue = $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').val();
                    // Pruefen ob eine Zahl eingegeben wurde
                    if (!isNaN(parseFloat(checkValue)) && isFinite(checkValue)) {
                        // Preloader einblenden
                        $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').append(oplib.preloader('opidOwnLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader','ownLayerGalleryPreflightPreloader'));
                        // Hier kann der Bilderpfad zusammengebaut und dann gerendert werden
                        if (checkValue === 1) {
                            // Erstes Bild
                            $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src', currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1]);
                        } else {
                            // Alle anderen Bilder
                            $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src', currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1] + '&page=' + checkValue);
                        }
                    } else {
                        $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').val('');
                    }
                }
            },
			findAll: function (selector, jQueryObject) {
				return jQueryObject.filter(selector).add( jQueryObject.find(selector) );
			},
			flagLastGalleryElement: function(element) {
				$('div[data-hovered="active"]').removeAttr('data-hovered');
				$(element).parent().attr('data-hovered','active');
			},
            formatUrlParams: function(urlparams){
                var paramsAsString = "";
                Object.keys(urlparams).forEach(function(key){
                    paramsAsString += "&" + key + "=" + urlparams[key];
                })

                return paramsAsString;
            },
			getDataOwnDialog: function(element) {
				var ownDialogData = $(element).data('owndialog') || $(element).data('ownlayer'),
                    ownDialogSelector = $(element).data('selector'),
					ownDialogWidth,
					ownDialogHeight,
					ownDialogType,
					ownDialogModel,
					ownDialogHeadlineId,
                    ownDialogHeadline,
					ownDialogContentId,
                    ownDialogOverlayClose,
                    ownDialogOrderId,
					ownDialogUrl,
					ownDialogUrlParams;

				// Default-Werte
				if(typeof ownDialogData === 'undefined' || ownDialogData == '') {
					ownDialogType = 'default';
					ownDialogWidth = '500';
					ownDialogHeight = 'auto';
					ownDialogModel = '5';
					ownDialogHeadlineId = 'ownDialogHeadlineDefault';
                    ownDialogHeadline = '';
					ownDialogContentId = 'ownDialogContentDefault';
                    ownDialogOverlayClose = 'true';
                    ownDialogOrderId = '';
                    ownDialogUrl = 'op_ajax_content.htm',
					ownDialogUrlParams = {};
				} else {
					ownDialogType = ownDialogData.type;
					ownDialogWidth = ownDialogData.width;
					ownDialogModel = ownDialogData.model;
					ownDialogHeadlineId = ownDialogData.headlineId;
                    ownDialogHeadline = ownDialogData.headline;
					ownDialogContentId = ownDialogData.contentId;
                    ownDialogOverlayClose = ownDialogData.overlayClose;
                    ownDialogOrderId = ownDialogData.orderId;
					ownDialogUrl = ownDialogData.url,
					ownDialogUrlParams = ownDialogData.urlparams;
				}

				if(typeof ownDialogType === 'undefined' || ownDialogType == '') { ownDialogType = 'default'; }
				if(typeof ownDialogWidth === 'undefined' || ownDialogWidth == '') { ownDialogWidth = '500'; }
				if(typeof ownDialogHeight === 'undefined' || ownDialogHeight == '') { ownDialogHeight = 'auto'; }
				if(typeof ownDialogModel === 'undefined' || ownDialogModel == '') { ownDialogModel = '5'; }
				if(typeof ownDialogHeadlineId === 'undefined' || ownDialogHeadlineId == '') { ownDialogHeadlineId = 'ownDialogHeadlineDefault'; }
                if(typeof ownDialogHeadline === 'undefined' || ownDialogHeadline == '') { ownDialogHeadline = ''; }
				if(typeof ownDialogContentId === 'undefined' || ownDialogContentId == '') { ownDialogContentId = 'ownDialogContentDefault'; }
                if(typeof ownDialogOverlayClose === 'undefined' || ownDialogOverlayClose == '') { ownDialogOverlayClose = 'true'; }
                if(typeof ownDialogOrderId === 'undefined' || ownDialogOrderId == '') { ownDialogOrderId = ''; }
                if(typeof ownDialogUrl === 'undefined' || ownDialogUrl == '') { ownDialogUrl = 'op_ajax_content.htm'; }
				if(typeof ownDialogUrlParams === 'undefined' || ownDialogUrlParams == '') { ownDialogUrlParams = {}; }
                if(typeof ownDialogSelector === 'undefined' || ownDialogSelector == '') { ownDialogSelector = ''; }

				// Stornierungsprozess Bestelluebersicht
                if($(element).hasClass('opidOwnDialogCancelOrder')) {
                    return {'ownDialogWidth':ownDialogWidth, 'ownDialogHeight':ownDialogHeight , 'ownDialogType':ownDialogType, 'ownDialogHeadline':ownDialogHeadline, 'ownDialogContentId':ownDialogContentId, 'ownDialogModel':ownDialogModel, 'ownDialogOverlayClose':ownDialogOverlayClose, 'ownDialogOrderId':ownDialogOrderId, 'ownDialogUrl':ownDialogUrl, 'ownDialogUrlParams': ownDialogUrlParams};
                } else {
                    return {'ownDialogWidth':ownDialogWidth, 'ownDialogHeight':ownDialogHeight , 'ownDialogType':ownDialogType, 'ownDialogHeadlineId':ownDialogHeadlineId, 'ownDialogContentId':ownDialogContentId, 'ownDialogModel':ownDialogModel, 'ownDialogOverlayClose':ownDialogOverlayClose, 'ownDialogUrl':ownDialogUrl, 'ownDialogUrlParams':ownDialogUrlParams, 'ownDialogSelector':ownDialogSelector};
                }
			},
			getDataOwnLayer: function(element) {
				var notFromLink = $(element).data('ownlayer').notFromLink,
                    ownLayerData,
					ownLayerWidth,
					ownLayerHeight;

                ownLayerData = $(element).data('ownlayer');

                // Default-Werte
                if(typeof ownLayerData === 'undefined' || ownLayerData == '') {
                    ownLayerWidth = '975';
                    if(notFromLink != 'true') {
                        ownLayerHeight = '80%';
                    }
                } else {
                    ownLayerWidth  = ownLayerData.width;
                    ownLayerHeight = ownLayerData.height;
                }

				
				if(typeof ownLayerWidth === 'undefined' || ownLayerWidth == '' || ownLayerWidth == 'Default') { ownLayerWidth = '975'; }
				if(typeof ownLayerHeight === 'undefined' || ownLayerHeight == '') {
                    if(notFromLink != 'true') {
                        ownLayerHeight = '80%';
                    }
                }

				return {'ownLayerWidth':ownLayerWidth, 'ownLayerHeight':ownLayerHeight};
			},
			// STARTPUNKT FUER EINEN NEUEN LAYER
			getSetOwnLayerDialogValues: function(element) {

                var element = element,
					imageDimensionsArr = [],
                    quantityElements,
					elementIndex,
					elementSource,
					elementRel,
					ownLayerType,
                    notFromLink;


				// Falls es Ã¼ber die Preview aufgerufen wird
				if($(element).hasClass('productHeadLeftPreview')) {
					elementIndex = $('.opadItem[data-hovered="active"]').index();
					element = $('.opadItem').eq(elementIndex).children('a');
				} else if($(element).hasClass('productPagePreview')) {
					// Falls es Ã¼ber die Preview aufgerufen wird - Mit JSON gebaut
                    elementIndex = $('.productPageItem[data-hovered="active"]').index();
                    element = $('.productPageItem').eq(elementIndex).children('a');
                } else if($(element).hasClass('opidPreflightUploaderPreviewThumb')) {
                    // Galerie fuer den PreflightUploader
                    element = $(element).parent('a');
                }


				elementSource = $(element).attr('href');
				ownLayerType = $(element).attr('id') || ( $(element).data('ownlayer') || {}).ownLayerType;
				elementRel = $(element).attr('rel');
				
				ownLayer.initializeOwnLayer();


				// GALERIE
				if(elementRel === 'opadCBoxGal' || elementRel === 'productPageCBoxGal') {
					var img = new Image();
					img.onload = function () {
						imageDimensionsArr.push(this.width);
						imageDimensionsArr.push(this.height);
						// Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
						ownLayer.renderOwnLayerGalleryContent(element);
						// Aufbau des Galerie Layers
						ownLayer.buildOwnGalleryLayer(imageDimensionsArr);
						// Zentriert den Layer
						//ownLayer.centerOwnLayer();
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
						// Anzeigen des Layers
						ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
						// Setzen einer Klasse als Kennzeichen fÃ¼r einen "Galerie-Layer"
						ownLayer.setClassForGallery();
						// Berechnen der Kopfzeile des Layers
						ownLayer.calculateOwnLayerHeader();
						// Zentrieren der Galerie-Navigation
						ownLayer.verticalCenterOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
						// Anzeigen der Galerie-Navigation
						ownLayer.showOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
						// Flag aktuelles Element
						ownLayer.flagLastGalleryElement(element);
						// Rendert den Inhalt in die PicInfo
						ownLayer.renderPicInfoContent();
						// Positionieren der Bilderzusatzinformation
						ownLayer.posOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo('default');
						// Anzeigen der Bilderzusatzinformation
						ownLayer.showOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo();
						// Legt zwei Handler ueber den Content
						ownLayer.posOwnLayerContentGalleryContent(imageDimensionsArr);
					};
					img.src = elementSource;
				}
//console.log('getSetOwnLayerDialogValues element = ',element);
                // GALERIE - JSON
                if(elementRel === 'productPageGal') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').addClass('ownLayerDefaultSizeGallery');
                    $('#ownLayerBodyWrapper').addClass('globalPositionRelative');
					imageDimensionsArr.push(this.width);
					imageDimensionsArr.push(this.height);
					countGalleryImages = $('.productPageItem').length,
					// Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
					ownLayer.renderOwnLayerGalleryContentJSON(element);
					// Aufbau des Galerie Layers
					ownLayer.buildOwnGalleryLayer(imageDimensionsArr);
					// Zentriert den Layer
					//ownLayer.centerOwnLayer();
					ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
					// Anzeigen des Layers
					ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
					// Setzen einer Klasse als Kennzeichen fÃ¼r einen "Galerie-Layer"
					ownLayer.setClassForGallery();
					// Berechnen der Kopfzeile des Layers
					ownLayer.calculateOwnLayerHeader();
					// Zentrieren der Galerie-Navigation
					ownLayer.verticalCenterOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
					// Anzeigen der Galerie-Navigation
					if(countGalleryImages > 1) {
						ownLayer.showOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
					}
					// Flag aktuelles Element
					ownLayer.flagLastGalleryElement(element);
					// Rendert den Inhalt in die PicInfo
					ownLayer.renderPicInfoContentJSON();
					// Positionieren der Bilderzusatzinformation
					ownLayer.posOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo('default');
					// Anzeigen der Bilderzusatzinformation
					ownLayer.showOwnLayerGalleryPicInfo();
					// Legt zwei Handler ueber den Content
					ownLayer.posOwnLayerContentGalleryContentJSON(imageDimensionsArr);
                }
                // Preview - Bestelluebersicht
                if(elementRel === 'orderOverviewPrev') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').addClass('ownLayerOrderOverviewDefaultSizePreview');
                    $('#ownLayerBodyWrapper').addClass('globalPositionRelative');
                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').addClass('ownLayerOrderOverviewPreview opidOwnLayerOrderOverviewPreview');
                    // Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
                    ownLayer.renderOwnLayerOrderOverviewPreviewContent(element);
                    // Aufbau des Galerie Layers
                    $('.opidOwnLayerOrderOverviewPreview .ownLayerBodyWrapper').css({'width':'400'}).addClass('globalTextAlignCenter');
                    $('.opidOwnLayerOrderOverviewPreview').css({'width':'430','height':'485'});
                    // Zentriert den Layer
                    ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
                    // Anzeigen des Layers
                    ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
                    // Zentriert den Uploader fuer die PreflightGallery
                    ownLayer.centerLayerPreloader('ownLayerPreloaderBody');
                    // Berechnen der Kopfzeile des Layers
                    ownLayer.calculateOwnLayerHeader();
                    $('.ownLayerBodyHandles').hide();
                    $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').attr('readonly','readonly');
                }
                // GALERIE - PreflightUploader
                if(elementRel === 'preflightUploaderGal') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').addClass('ownLayerDefaultSizeGallery');
                    $('#ownLayerBodyWrapper').addClass('globalPositionRelative');
                    quantityElements = parseInt($(element).attr('uploadpages'));
                    imageDimensionsArr.push(this.width);
                    imageDimensionsArr.push(this.height);
                    // Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
                    ownLayer.renderOwnLayerPreflightGalleryContent(element);
                    // Aufbau des Galerie Layers
                    ownLayer.buildOwnGalleryLayer(imageDimensionsArr);
                    // Zentriert den Layer
                    ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
                    // Anzeigen des Layers
                    ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
                    // Zentriert den Uploader fuer die PreflightGallery
                    ownLayer.centerLayerPreloader('ownLayerPreloaderBody');
                    // Setzen einer Klasse als Kennzeichen fÃ¼r einen "Galerie-Layer"
                    ownLayer.setClassBindClickForPreflightGallery();
                    // Berechnen der Kopfzeile des Layers
                    ownLayer.calculateOwnLayerHeader();
                    // Zentrieren der Galerie-Navigation
                    ownLayer.verticalCenterOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
                    if(quantityElements > 1) {
                        $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').removeAttr('readonly');
                        // Anzeigen der Galerie-Navigation
                        ownLayer.showOwnLayerGalleryNavigation();
                        // Legt zwei Handler ueber den Content
                        ownLayer.posOwnLayerContentGalleryContentJSON(imageDimensionsArr);
                    } else {
                        $('.ownLayerBodyHandles').hide();
                        $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').attr('readonly','readonly');
                    }
                }
				switch (ownLayerType) {
					// NORMALER LAYER
					case 'ownLayer' :
						// Holen der Daten aus dem "data-Objekt" des Trigger-Elements
						var dataOwnLayer = ownLayer.getDataOwnLayer(element);
						// Aufbau des Layers
						ownLayer.buildOwnLayer(dataOwnLayer);
						// Zentriert den Layer
						//ownLayer.centerOwnLayer();
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
						// Anzeigen des Layers
						ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
						
                        notFromLink = $(element).data('ownlayer').notFromLink;
                        // Holt den Contentn nicht aus einem Link
                        if(notFromLink == 'true') {
                            ownLayer.renderOwnLayerContentNotFromLink(element);
                        } else {
                            // Setzt die HÃ¶he des Contents
                            ownLayer.setContentHeight();
                            // Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
                            ownLayer.renderOwnLayerContent(element);
                        }
						break;
                    case 'ownLayerJSON' :
                        // Holen der Daten aus dem "data-Objekt" des Trigger-Elements
                        var dataOwnLayer = ownLayer.getDataOwnLayer(element);
                        // Aufbau des Layers
                        ownLayer.buildOwnLayer(dataOwnLayer);
                        // Zentriert den Layer
                        //ownLayer.centerOwnLayer();
                        ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
                        // Anzeigen des Layers
                        ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
                        // Setzt die HÃ¶he des Contents
                        ownLayer.setContentHeight();
                        // Rendert den JSON Inhalt fuer die Preflight-Zwischenseite
                        ownLayer.renderOwnLayerJSONContentPreflightLog(element);
                        break;
					case 'ownDialog' :
						// Holen der Daten aus dem "data-Objekt" des Trigger-Elements
						var dataOwnDialog = ownLayer.getDataOwnDialog(element);
						// Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
						ownLayer.renderOwnDialogContent(dataOwnDialog, element);
						// Aufbau des Dialog - Fusszeile - Buttons
						ownLayer.buildOwnDialogModel(dataOwnDialog);
						// Aufbau des Dialog - Typ des Dialogs (z.B. delete)
						ownLayer.buildOwnDialogType(element, dataOwnDialog);
						// Zentriert den Dialog
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
						// Anzeigen des Dialog
						ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
						break;
                    case 'ownDialogCancelOrder' :
                        // Holen der Daten aus dem "data-Objekt" des Trigger-Elements
                        var dataOwnDialog = ownLayer.getDataOwnDialog(element);


                        // Rendert den Inhalt in den Layer
                        //ownLayer.renderOwnDialogContent(dataOwnDialog, element);
                        ownLayer.renderOwnDialogContentCancelOrder(dataOwnDialog, element);


                        // Aufbau des Dialog - Fusszeile - Buttons
                        ownLayer.buildOwnDialogModel(dataOwnDialog);
                        // Aufbau des Dialog - Typ des Dialogs (z.B. delete)
                        ownLayer.buildOwnDialogType(element, dataOwnDialog);
                        // Zentriert den Dialog
                        ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
                        // Anzeigen des Dialog
                        ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
                        break;
					case 'ownDialogBasket' :
						// Holen der Daten aus dem "data-Objekt" des Trigger-Elements
						var dataOwnDialog = ownLayer.getDataOwnDialog(element);
						// Rendert die Ãœberschrift in den Layer
						ownLayer.buildOwnDialogHeadline(dataOwnDialog);
						// Aufbau des Dialog - Fusszeile - Buttons
						ownLayer.buildOwnDialogModel(dataOwnDialog);
						// Aufbau des Dialog - Typ des Dialogs (z.B. delete)
						ownLayer.buildOwnDialogType(element, dataOwnDialog);
						// Zentriert den Dialog
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
						// Anzeigen des Dialog
						ownLayer.showOwnLayer();
						break;
				}
			},
			getTrustedShopRating: function() {
				var _tsid = 'X1BB150C9C17CFDF5BC3571731CF6BEA1';
				var _ts = document.createElement('script');
					_ts.type = 'text/javascript';
					_ts.charset = 'utf-8';
					_ts.async = true; 
					_ts.src = '//widgets.trustedshops.com/js/' + _tsid + '.js'; 
					var __ts = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
					__ts.parentNode.insertBefore(_ts, __ts);	
			},

            preloadGalleryImages: function(element) {
                var totalImages = $(element).parent('a').attr('uploadpages'),
                    imageLink = $(element).parent('a').attr('href'),
                    images = new Array(),
                    i=0;
console.log('totalImages = ',totalImages);
console.log('imageLink = ',imageLink);

                for(; i<totalImages; i++) {
                    images[i] = new Image();
                    if(i === 0) {
                        // Das erste Bild muss ohne den Parameter page geladen werden
                        images[i].src = imageLink;
                    } else {
                        images[i].src = imageLink+'&page='+(i+1);
                    }
                }
            },
            removeLastCharacter: function() {
                $('.opidOwnLayerGalleryPicInfoCurrent').val(
                    function(index, value) {
                        // loescht den letzten Wert aus dem input
                        return value.substr(0, value.length - 1);
                    }
                )
            },
            restrictImageNumberInput: function(e) {
                var isNumber = e.keyCode >= 48 && e.keyCode <= 57 || e.keyCode >= 96 && e.keyCode <= 105,
                    isBackspace = e.keyCode === 8,
                    isArrows = e.keyCode === 37 || e.keyCode === 39,
                    isEnter = e.keyCode === 13,
                    isDelete = e.keyCode === 46;

                if(e.keyCode === 16) {
                    ownLayer.pressedShift = true;
                };
                if(e.keyCode === 17) {
                    ownLayer.pressedStrg = true;
                };
                if(e.keyCode === 18) {
                    ownLayer.pressedAlt = true;
                };

                if(!isNumber && !isBackspace && !isArrows && !isEnter && !isDelete) {

                    e.preventDefault();
                } else if(ownLayer.pressedShift || ownLayer.pressedStrg || ownLayer.pressedAlt) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                }
            },

                if(emailValidation('#shareUploadLinkForm')) {

                                '</div><div class="clear"></div>');

                    });
                }

			renderOwnDialogContent: function(elementData, element) {
			

				// Leert den bereits aufgerufenen Content aus dem Layer
				$('#ownLayerBody').html('');
                $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').removeAttr('data-selector');

				var ownDialogHeadlineId = elementData.ownDialogHeadlineId,
					ownDialogContentId = elementData.ownDialogContentId,
					ownDialogType = elementData.ownDialogType,
					urlparams = ownLayer.formatUrlParams(elementData.ownDialogUrlParams),

					
						repairedURL = oplib.characterRepair('https://de.onlineprinters.ch/'+elementData.ownDialogUrl+'?websale8=diedruckerei.15-aa'+urlparams+'&tpl='+elementData.ownDialogUrl);
					

					

				if(elementData.ownDialogSelector != '') {
                    $('.opidOwnLayerWrapper').attr('data-selector', elementData.ownDialogSelector);
                }

				// Alle Dialogs sollen keine Scrollbar bekommen
				$('#ownLayerBody').css('overflow','visible');
					
				// Preloader
				ownLayer.showPreloaderOwnDialog();
				
				ownLayer.centerElement('ownAjaxPreloader');	
				$('.ownLayerHeaderLeft').load(repairedURL+' #'+ownDialogHeadlineId, function() {
					// Hier kann der Preloader ausgeblendet werden, besser aber im Content-Bereich	
				});
				// 1. Ausnahme fuer den Dialog Direktlink
				if(ownDialogHeadlineId === 'ownDialogHeadlineDeepLink') {
//console.log('Bin in der Ausnahme 1.');
					$('#shortLinkColorBoxContent').appendTo($('#ownLayerBody'));
				// 2. Ausnahme fuer den Dialog XML-Bestellgenerator | umbennen
				} else if(ownDialogHeadlineId === 'ownDialogHeadlineXMLConfiguratorRename') {
//console.log('Bin in der Ausnahme 2.');
                    var repairedXMLURL = oplib.characterRepair($(element).attr('href'));
                    $('#ownLayerBody').addClass('dynamicXMLDialog').load(repairedXMLURL, function () {
                        ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
                        ownLayer.hidePreloaderOwnDialog();
                    });
                //  3. Ausnahme fuer den Dialog GeoIP
                } else if(ownDialogHeadlineId === 'ownDialogHeadlineGeoIpRedirect') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').html($('.'+ownDialogContentId).html());
                    ownLayer.hidePreloaderOwnDialog();
				//  4. Ausnahme fuer den Versandsplit Dialog
                } else if(ownDialogContentId === 'ownDialogContentMultiDelivery') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').html($('.'+ownDialogContentId).html());
                //  5. Ausnahme fuer den Paket-Tracking Dialog
                } else if(ownDialogContentId === 'ownDialogContentParcelTracking') {
                    $('#ownLayerBody').html($('.'+ownDialogContentId).html());
				// Standardverhalten
				} else {
//console.log('Bin keine Ausnahme');

					// Hier kann der Preloader eingeblendet werden
					$('#ownLayerBody').load(repairedURL+' #'+ownDialogContentId, function(){
						// Umbenennen XML-Produktkonfigurator
						if(ownDialogType == 'renameXMLConfigurator') {
							
							var oldSetId = $.trim($(element).parent().siblings().text());
							$('input[name="oldSetId"]').val(oldSetId);
						} else if(ownDialogType == 'shareUploadLink') {
                            
                            
						    // Schreibt den Share Upload-Link in den Dialog
                            
                            
                            	var orderId = '';
                            	
                            

							var repairedUrl = oplib.characterRepair('https://de.onlineprinters.ch/tpl_uploader_preflightcheck_op_positions-htm.htm?websale8=diedruckerei.15-aa&amp;tpl=tpl_uploader_preflightcheck_op_positions.htm&otp1='+orderId+'&otp2=1d4478b5149e363ae54450eb321af04c0c0409481b21d2bc32b57f2943f028fb392af49c009395ceb276a20a094dc5d057b5cedbc5910c84fa6b6ea27cae2430&puMode=4');
                            $('textarea#shareUploadLinkLinkLong').val(repairedUrl);
                            $('#shareUploadLinkLinkShow').html(repairedUrl);
                        // Sonderfall Preflight Uploader - hochgeladene Datei lÃ¶schen
						} else if(ownDialogType == 'deleteUpload') {
                            var data = $(element).data('owndialog') || $(element).data('ownlayer'),
                                uploadFilename = data.uploadFilename,
                                contentDialogDeleteUpload = '<b>'+uploadFilename+'</b>';
                            $(contentDialogDeleteUpload).appendTo('#ownDialogContentDeleteUploadTxt');
                        }
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownLayerWrapper');
						// Ausnahme Merkliste. Bei Merklisten-Dialog wird der Preloader Ã¼ber die ws-design-1.8.0.js ausgeblendet
						if(ownDialogType != 'memolist') {
							ownLayer.hidePreloaderOwnDialog();
						}
					});
				}
			},

            resizeGalleryImage: function() {
                var maxImageWidth = 570,
                    maxImageHeight = 545,
                    ratio = 0,
                    currentImageWidth,
                    currentImageHeight;

                    currentImageWidth = $('#ownLayerBody img').width();
                    currentImageHeight = $('#ownLayerBody img').height();

                if(currentImageWidth > maxImageWidth){
                    ratio = maxImageWidth / currentImageWidth;
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').css("width", maxImageWidth);
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').css("height", currentImageHeight * ratio);
                    currentImageHeight = currentImageHeight * ratio;
                    currentImageWidth = currentImageWidth * ratio;
                }
                if(currentImageHeight > maxImageHeight){
                    ratio = maxImageHeight / currentImageHeight;
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').css("height", maxImageHeight);
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').css("width", currentImageWidth * ratio);
                    currentImageWidth = currentImageWidth * ratio;
                    currentImageHeight = currentImageHeight * ratio;
                }
                $('#ownLayerBody img').css({
                    'width': currentImageWidth,
                    'height': currentImageHeight
                });
                $('#ownLayerBody img').show();
            },
            renderOwnLayerGalleryContentJSON: function(element) {
                var headerContent   = $('#productPageDescriptionTitle').text(),
                    footerContent   = $(element).attr('title'),
					fileId 			= $(element).attr('data-fileid'),
                    videoSrc        = $(element).attr('data-videosrc'),
					countGalleryImages = $('.productPageItem').length,

                    if(videoSrc != "") {
                        // Es handelt sich um ein Video
                        renderVideo = '<div class="ownLayerVideoWrapper">';

                        $('#ownLayerBody').html(renderVideo);
						$('.ownLayerBodyLeft, .ownLayerBodyRight').hide();
                    } else {
                        // Es handelt sich um ein Bild
                        newBodyContent = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch/$WS/diedruckerei/websale8_shop-diedruckerei/benutzer/templates/ws-customer/externe_daten/opADL/files/images/products/500x500/'+fileId;
                        newBodyContentRetina = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch/$WS/diedruckerei/websale8_shop-diedruckerei/benutzer/templates/ws-customer/externe_daten/opADL/files/images/products/1000x1000/'+fileId;
                        renderPicture =  '<picture>';
                        renderPicture += '<source type="image/webp" srcset="'+newBodyContent+'.webp 1x, '+newBodyContentRetina+'.webp 2x">';
                        renderPicture += '<img src="'+newBodyContent+'.jpg" srcset="'+newBodyContent+'.jpg 1x, '+newBodyContentRetina+'.jpg 2x" alt="">';
                        renderPicture += '</picture>';	

                        $('#ownLayerBody').html(renderPicture);

						if(countGalleryImages > 1){
							$('.ownLayerBodyLeft, .ownLayerBodyRight').show();
						}
                    }




                $('.ownLayerHeaderLeft').html(headerContent);
                $('.ownLayerFooter').html(footerContent);
            },

			renderOwnLayerContent: function(element) {
				// Trigger Layer: <div class="formInfoBoxReadOnly formInfoBoxReadOnlyGrey" data-ownlayer="{"ownLayerType":"ownLayer","width":"970","height":""}">
				var elementLink,
					ownLayerHeadlineId,
                    ownLayerContentId,
					elementRel,
					ownURL,
					repairedURLContent,
					repairedURLHeadline,
					dataForTemplatesElement,
                    isImage = '',
                    isChangeImage = false;


					elementLink = $(element).closest('a').attr('href');
					ownLayerHeadlineId = $(element).data('ownlayer').ownLayerHeadlineId;
					ownLayerContentId = $(element).data('ownlayer').ownLayerContentId;
					isImage = $(element).data('ownlayer').image;
					isChangeImage =  $(element).data('ownlayer').changeImage;
					
					repairedURLContent = oplib.characterRepair(elementLink);

					elementRel = $(element).parent().attr('rel');
					ownURL = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch';
                    //ownURL = 'https://www.diedruckerei.de';
					//repairedURLContent = oplib.characterRepair(elementLink);
                    repairedURLHeadline = oplib.characterRepair('https://de.onlineprinters.ch/op_ajax_content-htm.htm?websale8=diedruckerei.15-aa&amp;tpl=op_ajax_content.htm');


					// Leert den Content des Layers
					$('#ownLayerBody').html('');

					
					
					var CatIndexs = ['003293','003294','003295','003296','003297','005652','003299','003298','005653'],
					    isInternalURL = repairedURLContent.indexOf(ownURL) > -1,
						isCalender = $.inArray('',CatIndexs) > -1,
						isBtnDownload = $(element).parent().hasClass('btnDownload'),
						isCalenderBtnDownload = (isCalender && isBtnDownload);

                    if ((isInternalURL && !isCalenderBtnDownload) || isChangeImage) {

						

						
						
						
						

						
						// Preloader
						ownLayer.showPreloaderOwnLayer();
						ownLayer.centerLayerPreloader();
						// Holen der einzelnen Elemente via AJAX fÃ¼r den Layer
						if ((ownLayerHeadlineId === undefined) || (ownLayerHeadlineId == null) || (ownLayerHeadlineId == "undefined")) {
							// Es wurde keine ownLayerHeadlinId uebergeben
                        
						    } else if (isImage) {
                        
                        
                            $('#ownLayerHeaderLeft').html(ownLayerHeadlineId);
                        } else {
							$('.ownLayerHeaderLeft').load(repairedURLHeadline + ' #' + ownLayerHeadlineId, function () {
								// Hier kann der Preloader ausgeblendet werden, besser aber erst nach erfolgreichem laden des Contents
							});
						}

						if (typeof elementRel == 'undefined') {
							elementRel = 'Default';
						}

						// Wenn image = true dann Bild zentrieren
						if (isImage == "true") {
							ownLayer.renderOwnLayerContentImage(elementLink, 'fitLayer');
						} else {
							
						}

						// Sonderfall fuer Produktvorschau oder um ein Bild handelt z.B. Zertifikate

                        if (elementRel.indexOf('gallery_') > -1 || isImage) {
                            ownLayer.renderOwnLayerContentImage(elementLink, 'default');
                        } else if (isChangeImage) {
                            ownLayer.renderOwnLayerContentImage(elementLink, 'fitLayer');
						} else if (ownLayerHeadlineId === 'ownLayerHeadlineTemplateDownload') {
							productPageObject.getJSONDataForTemplates(element);
						} else {
							
							$.ajax({
								beforeSend: function (xhr) {
									
									xhr.overrideMimeType("text/html;charset=ISO-8859-1");
								},
								type: 'get',
								url: repairedURLContent,
								success: function (response) {
									
									var partialResponse,
										result = $(response);

									partialResponse = ownLayer.findAll('[data-ownlayercontent="ownlayercontent"]', result);

									if (partialResponse.length < 1 && ownLayer.findAll('.neos-contentcollection', result).length > 0) {
										partialResponse = ownLayer.findAll('#content', result);
										ownLayer.modifyNeosContent( partialResponse );
									}

									if (partialResponse.length < 1) {
										partialResponse = result;
									}

									

                                    if(ownLayerHeadlineId === 'ownLayerHeadlineTemplateDownload') {
                                        partialResponse = ownLayer.findAll('#ownLayerContentTemplateDownload', result);
                                    } else if(ownLayerContentId === 'ownLayerContentDatacheck') {
                                        partialResponse = ownLayer.findAll('#ownLayerContentDatacheck', result);
                                    }

									$('#ownLayerBody').append(partialResponse);

									var opAjaxHeadline = $('#ownLayerHeaderLeft').text(),
										contentHeadline;
									// Setzen des Headers, falls nicht schon einer vorhanden ist
									contentHeadline = $('#ownLayerBody').find('h1').text();
									if (!opAjaxHeadline) {
										$('#ownLayerHeaderLeft').text(contentHeadline);
									}

									$('#ownLayerBody').find('[dataownlayerheadline="ownlayerheadline"], [data-ownlayerheadline="ownlayerheadline"]').hide();

									$('div[data-ownlayercontent="ownlayercontent"]').css({
										'width': 'auto',
										'margin': 0,
										'padding': 0,
										'padding-right': '15px'
									});

									ownLayer.hidePreloaderOwnLayer();
								}
							});
						}
						$('#ownLayerBody').css('overflow-y', 'auto');
					} else {
						// IFrame
						
						// Hier kann der Preloader eingeblendet werden
						ownLayer.showPreloader();
						ownLayer.centerElement('ownAjaxPreloader');

						// Rendern der Headline in den ownLayer
						$('#ownLayerHeaderLeft').load(repairedURLHeadline + ' #' + ownLayerHeadlineId, function () {
							// Hier kann der Preloader ausgeblendet werden, besser aber erst nach erfolgreichem laden des Contents
						});

						$('#ownLayerBody').append('<iframe id="ownLayerIframe" src="' + elementLink + '"></iframe>')
						// Hier kann der Preloader ausgeblendet werden - setTimeout ist nur zu Testzwecken!
						//setTimeout('ownLayer.hidePreloader()', 100);

						ownLayer.hidePreloader();

					}
				},
                renderOwnLayerJSONContentPreflightLog: function(element) {

					var elementData = $(element).data('ownlayer'),
					    elementIndex = elementData.elementIndex,
                        elementHeadline = elementData.ownLayerHeadlineId,
                        elementStatus = ownLayer.preflightUploaderResponse.positions[elementIndex].status,
                        elementUploadsLength = ownLayer.preflightUploaderResponse.positions[elementIndex].uploads.length,
                        ownLayerJSONBodyContent,
                        elementUploadFile,
                        i=0;

                    // Hier wird festgelegt ob es Datei oder Dateien lauten muss
                    if(elementUploadsLength == 1) {
                        elementUploadFile = 'Datei';
                    } else {
                        elementUploadFile = 'Dateien';
                    }

                    $('#ownLayerHeaderLeft').text(elementHeadline);

                    // Gruene Box oben

                        if(elementStatus == '15') {
                            ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<p class="ownLayerJSONText">Es sind keine weiteren Uploads mehr möglich.</p>';
                            ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<p class="ownLayerJSONText">Sie haben Ihren Datenupload vollständig abgeschlossen. Vielen Dank!</p>';
                        } else if (elementStatus == '14') {
                            ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<p class="ownLayerJSONText">Sie haben bereits Druckdaten hochgeladen, den Datenupload jedoch noch nicht vollständig abgeschlossen. Bitte nehmen Sie die fehlenden Schritte vor.</p>';
                        }
                    ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '</div>';

                    ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<p class="ownLayerJSONUploadHeadline globalFontWeightBold">Durchgeführte Übertragungen</p>';
                    ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div id="ownLayerJSONContentLog" class="ownLayerJSONContentLog">';
                        ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="globalDisplayTable">';
                            for(;i<elementUploadsLength;i++) {
                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="globalDisplayTableRow">';
                                        ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="opidOwnLayerJSONContentLogEndOfUpload ownLayerJSONContentLogEndOfUpload globalDisplayTableCell">'+ownLayer.preflightUploaderResponse.positions[elementIndex].uploads[i].endOfUpload+'</div>';

                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '</div>';

                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="globalDisplayTableRow">';

                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="ownLayerJSONContentLogAction globalDisplayTableCell">Upload abgeschlossen</div>';
                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '<div class="ownLayerJSONContentLogName globalDisplayTableCell">' + elementUploadsLength + ' ' + elementUploadFile + '</div>';
                                ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '</div>';
                            }
                        ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '</div>';

                    ownLayerJSONBodyContent += '</div>';

                    $('#ownLayerBody').append(ownLayerJSONBodyContent);
                    preflightUploaderPositionsObj.formatMomentDateFormat();

                },

			open: function(config){
				if(config == undefined)
					throw new Error('ownLayer.run(config) => config is undefined');

				var dataOwnLayerAttribute = config['data-ownlayer'];
				if(dataOwnLayerAttribute)
				{
					//Alle Werte werden String benÃ¶tigt ansonsten geht der ownlayer nicht
					$.each(Object.keys(dataOwnLayerAttribute),function(i,key){
						if(dataOwnLayerAttribute[key] != null && typeof dataOwnLayerAttribute[key] !== 'string')
						{
							dataOwnLayerAttribute[key] = JSON.stringify(dataOwnLayerAttribute[key]);
						}		
					})
					config['data-ownlayer'] = JSON.stringify(dataOwnLayerAttribute)
				}

				

				var element = $('<div/>', config)
                ownLayer.runNormalDialogDevelopment(element);
			},

            // Preflight Uploader
            showNextPreflightGalleryElementLayer: function() {
                var quantityElements = $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('uploadpages'),
                    currentImageSrc = $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src'),
                    currentImageSrcParts = currentImageSrc.split('&'),
                    newImageSource;
                if(quantityElements > 1) {
                    if (currentImageSrc.indexOf('page=') === -1) {
                        // Es ist das erste Bild
                        newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1] + '&page=2';
                    } else {
                        // Es ist nicht das erste Bild
                        var activeElementNumber = currentImageSrc.substr(currentImageSrc.indexOf("page=") + 5);
                        if (quantityElements === activeElementNumber) {
                            // Es ist das letzte Bild zurÃ¼ck zum Ersten
                            newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1];
                        } else {
                            // Es gibt noch weitere Bilder
                            var newElementNumber = parseInt(activeElementNumber) + 1;
                            newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1] + '&page=' + newElementNumber;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src', newImageSource);
                }
            },
            showPreviousPreflightGalleryElementLayer: function() {
                var quantityElements = $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('uploadpages'),
                    currentImageSrc = $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src'),
                    currentImageSrcParts = currentImageSrc.split('&'),
                    newImageSource;
                if(quantityElements > 1) {
                    if (currentImageSrc.indexOf('page=') === -1) {
                        // Es ist das erste Bild
                        newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1] + '&page=' + quantityElements;
                    } else {
                        // Es ist nicht das erste Bild
                        var activeElementNumber = currentImageSrc.substr(currentImageSrc.indexOf("page=") + 5);
                        if (activeElementNumber === '2') {
                            // Es ist das zweite Bild zurÃ¼ck zum Ersten
                            newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1];
                        } else {
                            // Es gibt noch weitere Bilder
                            var newElementNumber = parseInt(activeElementNumber) - 1;
                            newImageSource = currentImageSrcParts[0] + '&' + currentImageSrcParts[1] + '&page=' + newElementNumber;
                        }
                    }
                    $('#ownLayerBody img').attr('src', newImageSource);
                }
            },

<script type="text/javascript">
var cookiejar = document.cookie;
var cookies = cookiejar.split("; ");
var mycookie = "";
for(var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++)
{
if(cookies[i].search('websale_ac') > -1) {
mycookie = cookies[i].substr(cookies[i].indexOf("=") + 1);
break;
}
}

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function() {
            trustedShopBadgeObj.fillStars();
        });

        trustedShopBadgeObj = {
            rating: '4.57',
            fillStars: function() {
                var ratingArr = trustedShopBadgeObj.rating.split('.'),
                    ratingStarsFull = ratingArr[0],
                    ratingStarsPart = ratingArr[1],
                    elementFullWidth = parseFloat($('.opidTrustedShopBadgeBGFull').css('width')),
                    totalDistArr = [4,3,4,3],
                    totalDist = 0,
                    totalStars = ratingStarsFull * 12,
                    partStar = 12 * (ratingStarsPart/100),
                    elementFullNewWidth,
                    i = 0;

                for(;i<ratingStarsFull;i++) {
                    totalDist += totalDistArr[i];
                }
                elementFullNewWidth = elementFullWidth + totalDist + totalStars + partStar;
                $('.opidTrustedShopBadgeBGFull').removeClass('globalDisplayNone').css('width',elementFullNewWidth);
            }
        };
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function() {

	$(document).on('mouseenter', '.opidOwnToolTipTrigger', function(event) {
		//console.log('Bin im hover');
			var triggerElement = $(event.currentTarget);
			var ownToolTipData = $(triggerElement).data('owntooltip');

			// Zuerst alle ToolTips entfernen
			ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
			var elementInfos = ownToolTipObject.initialize(triggerElement);
			ownToolTipObject.createOwnToolTipBoxRight(triggerElement, elementInfos, ownToolTipData);
	});
	$(document).on('mouseleave', '.opidOwnToolTipTrigger', function(event) {
		$('.opidOwnToolTipHoverContainer').hover(function(event) {
			$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').show();    
		}, function() {
			ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
		});
		ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
	});

    $('body').on('click', '.opidOwnToolTipMoreAnker', function(e) {
        var ownToolTipLayerDataObj = $(this).children('div').attr('data-ownlayer');
        if (typeof ownToolTipLayerDataObj !== typeof undefined && ownToolTipLayerDataObj !== false) {
            e.preventDefault();
            ownLayer.openLayerFromToolTip($(this).children());
            ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
        }
    });

	$(document).scroll(function() {
		ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
	});
});

$(document).ajaxSuccess(function() {
    
    
    if (typeof preFunctionHideSelectedAdditionalOption == 'function') {
        preFunctionHideSelectedAdditionalOption();
    }

    var triggerElement,
        triggerElementWidth,
        triggerElementHeight,
        ownToolTipData;
    
    $('.opidOwnToolTipTrigger').hover(function(event) {
        triggerElement = $(event.currentTarget);
        ownToolTipData = $(triggerElement).data('owntooltip');

        // Zuerst alle ToolTips entfernen
        ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
        var elementInfos = ownToolTipObject.initialize(triggerElement);
        ownToolTipObject.createOwnToolTipBoxRight(triggerElement, elementInfos, ownToolTipData);
    },function() {
        $('.ownToolTipHoverContainer').hover(function(event) {
            $('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').show();    
        }, function() {
            ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
        });
            ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
    });
    
    $('body').on('click', '.opidOwnToolTipMoreAnker', function() {
        ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
    });
    
    $(document).scroll(function() {
        ownToolTipObject.hideAllToolTips();
    });    
});

// Objekte und Methoden
var ownToolTipObject = {
    // ToolTip Rechts
    createOwnToolTipBoxRight: function(triggerElement, elementInfos, ownToolTipData) {
		var offset = $(triggerElement).offset(),
			width = $(triggerElement).width(),
            ownToolTipLeft = offset.left+elementInfos[0]+11,
            ownToolTipMore = ownToolTipData.more,
            ownToolTipWidth = ownToolTipData.width,
            ownToolTipHoverContainerWidth = 12,
            ownToolTipOffsetLeft = ownToolTipData.offsetLeft,
			ownToolTipHoverContainerLeft = ownToolTipLeft-12,
			ownToolTipClass = ownToolTipData.class,
			ownToolTipCenter = ownToolTipData.tooltipCenter,
            ownToolTipTop;

		if(typeof ownToolTipOffsetLeft === 'undefined' || ownToolTipOffsetLeft == '') {ownToolTipOffsetLeft = '0';}
		
		if(typeof ownToolTipClass === 'undefined' || ownToolTipClass == '') {ownToolTipClass = '';}
		if(ownToolTipClass) {
			$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').addClass(ownToolTipClass);
		} else {
			$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').removeClass().addClass('opidOwnToolTipWrapper ownToolTipWrapper globalDisplayNone');
		}

		if(typeof ownToolTipCenter === 'undefined' || ownToolTipCenter == '') {
			ownToolTipCenter = false;
			$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').removeClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterUp ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterDown');
		}

        // Damit Content hineingerendert werden kann
        $('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').css({
            'visibility' : 'hidden',
            'display'     : 'block'
        });

        // Hier wird die Breite Ã¼bergeben
		$('.ownToolTip').css('width', ownToolTipWidth);


		if(ownToolTipData.contenttext)
			$('.opidOwnToolTipContent span').html(ownToolTipData.contenttext);

		if(ownToolTipData.contentselector)
			$('.opidOwnToolTipContent span').html($(ownToolTipData.contentselector).html());

		if(ownToolTipData.contentopwsvars)
			$('.opidOwnToolTipContent span').html(window.opWsVars[ownToolTipData.contentopwsvars]);

        // Falls "Weitere Informationen" existieren
        if(ownToolTipMore) {
            ownToolTipObject.prepareMoreInfoLayerNoLayer(ownToolTipData.morelink, ownToolTipData.moretext, triggerElement);
            $('.opidOwnToolTipMoreAnker').show();
        } else {
            $('.opidOwnToolTipMoreAnker').hide();            
        }

        ownToolTipTop = ownToolTipObject.toolTipDirection(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset);

		ownToolTipHoverContainerLeft = parseInt(ownToolTipHoverContainerLeft)+parseInt(ownToolTipOffsetLeft);
		
		if(ownToolTipCenter) {
			ownToolTipHoverContainerLeft = offset.left + width / 2 - ownToolTipWidth / 2 -12;
		}

        $('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').css({
            'width'        : ownToolTipHoverContainerWidth,
            'left'        : ownToolTipHoverContainerLeft,
            'top'        : ownToolTipTop
        }).css('visibility','visible').show();
    },
    // Alle ToolTips entfernen
    hideAllToolTips: function() {
		$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').hide();
    },
    initialize: function(triggerElement) {
        triggerElementWidth = $(triggerElement).width();
        triggerElementHeight = $(triggerElement).height();
                
        return [triggerElementWidth, triggerElementHeight];
            
    },
    // Link auf neue Seite
    prepareMoreInfoLayerNoLayer: function(morelink, moretext, triggerElement) {
        var ownToolTipLayerDataObj = $(triggerElement).attr('data-ownlayer');
        if(morelink)
			$('.opidOwnToolTipMoreAnker').attr('href', morelink);  
		if(moretext)  
			$('.opidOwnTooltipMoreText').html(moretext);
		
        if (typeof ownToolTipLayerDataObj !== typeof undefined && ownToolTipLayerDataObj !== false) {
            $('.opidOwnToolTipMoreInfo').attr({'data-ownlayer':ownToolTipLayerDataObj, 'id':'ownLayer'})
        }else {
            $('.opidOwnToolTipMoreInfo').removeAttr('data-ownlayer').addClass('opidOwnToolTipMoreInfo');
        }    
    },
    toolTipDirection: function(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset) {
        
        var    windowHeight = parseInt($(window).height()),
            currentScrollPosTop = parseInt($(window).scrollTop()),
            windowTop = 37,
            toolTipBoxBottom;

        
        if(typeof ownToolTipData === 'undefined' || ownToolTipData == '') {
            ownToolTipDirection = 'down';
        } else {
            ownToolTipDirection  = ownToolTipData.direction;
        }
        if(typeof ownToolTipDirection === 'undefined' || ownToolTipDirection == '') {
            ownToolTipDirection = 'down';
        }
        
        if(ownToolTipDirection === 'up') {
            ownToolTipObject.toolTipDirectionUp(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset);
        } else if( ownToolTipDirection === 'down') {
            ownToolTipObject.toolTipDirectionDown(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset);
        }
        
        
        toolTipBoxBottom = ownToolTipTop + ($('.ownToolTip').height());

        if(windowHeight < toolTipBoxBottom - currentScrollPosTop) {
            console.log('Bin unten angekommen oder dar&uuml;ber hinaus, muss nach oben angezeigt werden');
            ownToolTipObject.toolTipDirectionUp(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset);
        }
        
        if(windowTop > ownToolTipTop - currentScrollPosTop) {
            console.log('Bin oben angekommen oder dar&uuml;ber hinaus, muss nach unten angezeigt werden');
            ownToolTipObject.toolTipDirectionDown(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset);
        }
        return ownToolTipTop;
    },
    toolTipDirectionUp: function(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset) {
		
        var toolTipBoxHeight = $('.ownToolTip').height(),
            ownToolTipTriggerHeight = $(triggerElement).height();
            ownToolTipTop = offset.top + 15 - toolTipBoxHeight + ownToolTipTriggerHeight/2;
			var ownToolTipCenter = ownToolTipData.tooltipCenter;
            $('.opidOwnToolTipHoverContainer').removeClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerDown').addClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerUp');
			if(typeof ownToolTipCenter === 'undefined' || ownToolTipCenter == '') {ownToolTipCenter = false;}
			if(ownToolTipCenter) {
				ownToolTipTop = offset.top - 30 - toolTipBoxHeight + ownToolTipTriggerHeight/2;
				$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').removeClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterDown').addClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterUp');
			}
    },

    toolTipDirectionDown: function(triggerElement, ownToolTipData, offset) {
        $('.opidOwnToolTipHoverContainer').removeClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerUp').addClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerDown');
        var ownToolTipTriggerHeight = $(triggerElement).height();
        ownToolTipTop = offset.top - 15 + ownToolTipTriggerHeight/2;
		var ownToolTipCenter = ownToolTipData.tooltipCenter;
		if(typeof ownToolTipCenter === 'undefined' || ownToolTipCenter == '') {ownToolTipCenter = false;}
		if(ownToolTipCenter) {
			ownToolTipTop = offset.top + 30 + ownToolTipTriggerHeight/2;
			$('.opidOwnToolTipWrapper').removeClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterUp').addClass('ownToolTipHoverContainerTriangleCenterDown');
		}
    }
};    
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
		//fÃ¼gt die Klasse touchHover hinzu, Ã¶ffnet SubmenÃ¼
		$(document).ready(function(event) {
			var touchHoverTimeout;
			$(document).on('touchstart', '.stickyHeaderMainNavLi', function(event){
				if ( $(event.target) !== $(this) ) {
					event.preventDefault();
				}

				var link = $(this);
				if (link.hasClass('touchHover')) {
					if ( !$(event.target).closest('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavJsonProduct, .stickyHeaderMainNavSub').length ) {
						link.removeClass('touchHover');
						$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeOut(150);
					}
					return true;
					} 
				else {
					link.addClass('touchHover');
					$('ul > li').not(this).removeClass('touchHover');
					$(".stickyHeaderMainNavUl li ul").css("display", "none");
					$(".stickyHeaderMainNavUl li").css("background", "none");
					$(".stickyHeaderMainNavSub ul li:hover").css({ "color":"#464646","content":"none","background":"none !important"});
					$(".stickyHeaderMainNavProduct .level1 li:hover").css({ "color":"#464646","content":"none","background":"none !important"});
					$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeIn(150);
					return false;
				}
			});
			$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavJsonProduct, .opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').on("touchstart", function (event) {
				event.stopPropagation();
			});
			$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch').on("touchstart", function (event) {
				if ( !$(event.target).closest('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').length ) {
					if ( $('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch').hasClass('touchHover') ) {
						$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').addClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
					} else {
						$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').removeClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
					}
				}
			});

            $(document).on('mouseenter', '.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLi', function() {
				touchHoverTimeout = setTimeout(function(){
					$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavUl ').addClass('touchHovering');
					$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeIn(150);
				}, 150);
            });

            $(document).on('mouseleave', '.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLi', function(event) {
				var currentElement = $(event.target).closest('li').attr('class');
				var currentParentLi = $(event.relatedTarget).closest('li.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLi').attr('class');
				var searchBoxInputFocus = $('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchInput').is(':focus');
				clearTimeout(touchHoverTimeout);

                if((currentParentLi == null) && (!searchBoxInputFocus)) {
					$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavUl ').removeClass('touchHovering');
                    $('.stickyHeaderOverlay').fadeOut(150);
                }
            });

		});
		//schlieÃŸt SubmenÃ¼ wenn auÃŸerhalb geklickt wird
		$(document).on("touchstart", function(event){
			if ( !$(event.target).closest('.stickyHeaderMainNavLi').length ) {
				$('ul > li').not(this).removeClass('touchHover');
				$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeOut(150);
			}
			if (!$(event.target).closest('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch, .opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').length) {
				if($('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').hasClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible')) {
					$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').removeClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
					$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeOut(150);
				}
			}    
		});

		//Ã–ffnet das SubmenÃ¼ nur wenn sich die Maus bewegt
		$(function() {
			var moveCount = 0;
			var touchHoverTimeout;
			$("html").mousemove(function () {
			if(moveCount++ === 0) return;
			$(function() {
				$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLi').hover(function (){
					var $MainNavLi = $(this);
					if ( $('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavUl').hasClass('touchHovering') ) {
						$MainNavLi.addClass("touchHover");
					} else {
						touchHoverTimeout = setTimeout(function(){
							$MainNavLi.find('ul').fadeIn(150);
							$MainNavLi.addClass("touchHover");
						}, 150);
					}
					if ( $('.opidStickyHeaderQuickWrapper').is(':visible') ) {
						$('.opidStickyHeaderQuickWrapper').css({'display':'none', 'opacity':'0'});
					}
					removeProductOptionsSelect();
				},function(){
					clearTimeout(touchHoverTimeout);
					$(this).find('ul').hide();
					$(this).removeClass("touchHover");
				});
			});
			$(this).unbind('mousemove');
			});
		});
		// Suche Main Menu
		
		
		$(document).ready(function() {
			var touchHoverTimeout;
			$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch').hover(function () {
				$('.stickyHeaderBig .opidStickyHeaderNavSearchAdditional').show();
				searchBoxAdditionalHover = $('.opidStickyHeaderNavSearchAdditional').is(':hover');
				if (!searchBoxAdditionalHover) {
					touchHoverTimeout = setTimeout(function(){
						$('.stickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').addClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
					}, 150);
				}
			}, function () {
				clearTimeout(touchHoverTimeout);
				$('.stickyHeaderBig .opidStickyHeaderNavSearchAdditional').hide();
				searchBoxInputFocus = $('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchInput').is(':focus');
				if (!searchBoxInputFocus) {
					$('.stickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').removeClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
				}
			});
			$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch').click(function () {
				setTimeout(function () {
					$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchInput').focus();
				}, 100);
			});
			$(document).click(function(event) { 
				if(!$(event.target).closest('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavLiSearch, .opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').length) {
					if($('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').hasClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible')) {
						$('.opidStickyHeaderMainNavSearchBarWrapper').removeClass('stickyHeaderMainNavSearchVisible');
						$('.opidStickyHeaderOverlay').fadeOut(150);
					}
				}        
			});
		});
		
		
	</script>
<div class="opidStickyHeaderMainNavBg stickyHeaderMainNavBg"></div>
