        $(document).ready(function() {
            var pageName = $('body').attr('id'),
                content;
            if(pageName === 'page_confirm') {
                $('#stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartProduct, #stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartBodySuff, .stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartIconOverlay').remove();
                $('#stickyHeaderSubNavImage').removeClass('iconsGeneral-shoppingCartMulticartActive').addClass('iconsGeneral-shoppingCart');

                content = '<div id="stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartHead" class="stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartHead stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartHeadEmpty stickyHeaderQuickBoxPadding">';
                    content += '<p class="stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartEmpty marginPaddingReset ">';
                        content += '<span id="stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCart" class="globalFontWeightBold">Ihr Warenkorb ist leer.</span>';
                    content += '</p>';
                content += '</div>';
                $('#stickyHeaderQuickshoppingCartContent').append(content);
            }
        });

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).on("ws_AJAXloadLoginResponseSuccess", function(){
            // PrÃ¼fen ob eingeloggt
            if ($('.opidStickyHeaderQuickAccountNavImageSuccess').length) {
                
                
                    // Hier ist man bereits eingeloggt somit kann der value gesetzt werden
                    ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'userStatus':'existing user','isLoggedIn':true});
                

                var url = 'https://de.onlineprinters.ch/websale8/?Ctx=%257bver%252f8%252fver%257d%257bst%252f415%252fst%257d%257bcmd%252f0%252fcmd%257d%257bm%252fwebsale%252fm%257d%257bs%252fdiedruckerei%252fs%257d%257bl%252f15%252daa%252fl%257d%257bmd5%252f87e28fe6088e68cdd9b0ab19cf944a66%252fmd5%257d';
                ws_AJAXloadTemplate(url, 'ISO-8859-1', ws_AJAXuserClassificationStart, ws_AJAXuserClassificationError, 'ws_AJAXuserClassificationResponseSuccess()', 'ws_AJAXuserClassificationResponseError()');
            } else {
                
                
                    ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'isLoggedIn':false});
                
            }
        });

<script type="text/javascript">
        if(typeof(op_w2p) === 'undefined') var op_w2p = '';

        $(document).ready(function() {
            var excludedTemplates = ['ws_logout'];
            if(excludedTemplates.includes(opWsVars.templateName)) {
                ga360Obj.removeItemLocalStorageGa360(ga360Obj.toRemoveFromLS);
            }
			ga360Obj.initCustomDimensions();
        });

        
        $('body').on('click', '.opidOwnDialogBtnToProduct, .opidOwnLayerBackgroundOverlay, .opidOwnLayerHeaderRight', function() {
            if(ga360Obj.addToBasketLayerOpen) {
                
                ga360Obj.pageMetaAndDimension();
                ga360Obj.addToBasketLayerOpen = false;
            }
        });

		/*
			Schreibt das geklickte Element und die vorhergehende Seite in den wsSessionStorage
		*/
		$(document).on('click', 'a,[el]', function (e) {
			var previousSite = ga360Obj.getPreviousSite();
			var clickedElement = ga360Obj.getClickedElement($(this));
			oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('previousSite', previousSite || "NULL");
			oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('clickedElement', clickedElement || "NO_CLICKID");
		});

		/*
			Schreibt die vorhergehende Seite beim Seitenunload in den wsSessionStorage
		*/
		$(window).on('unload', function (e) {
			var previousSite = ga360Obj.getPreviousSite();
			oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('previousSite', previousSite || "NULL");
		});

        
        $(document).on('click', '.opidSuggestionList, .opidTaglisttagDeleteIcon', function() {
            
            ga360Obj.pageMetaAndDimension();
        });

        
        $(document).on('click', '.opidVoucherDeleteBtn, .opidBasketVoucherDelete', function() {
            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'voucherCodeSet': false,'voucherCode':''});
        });

        
        $(document).on('click', '.opidMemoIntoBasket', function() {
            ga360Obj.memoIndex = parseInt($(this).attr('data-index'))-1;
        });

        
        $(document).on('click', '.opidDuplicateTrigger', function() {
            ga360Obj.isDuplicate = true;
			//ga360Obj.isBasketVoucher = false;
        });

		
		$(document).on('opProductionTimeCalculated', function (event, data) {
			if (typeof ga360Obj === 'undefined' || opWsVars.templateName == 'ws_confirm')
				return;

			if (!(['ws_address_combined'].includes(opWsVars.templateName))) {
				ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({
					'productionTime': data.products.map(function(entry){return entry.durationOfProductionWithAdditionalDays}),
					'deliveryTime': data.products.map(function(entry) {
						const fromTime = entry.shippingTimeFrom.daysWithShipping;
						const toTime = entry.deliveryDateHasRange ? entry.shippingTimeTo.daysWithShipping : 'X';
						return [fromTime, toTime];
					}),
					'expressDelivery': opWsVars.versandart.includes('EXPRESS'),
					'deliveryOption': opWsVars.versandid.replace(/.*?[\_-]/,'')
				});
			}

            
            if(ga360Obj.removeFromBasket) ga360Obj.removeFromCart();
            

            
            var templatesNotFireEmptyBasket = ['ws_address_combined', 'ws_pay_deliv', 'ws_place_order', 'ws_confirm'];
            if(templatesNotFireEmptyBasket.includes(opWsVars.templateName)) {
                
            } else {
				if(!ga360Obj.isDuplicate) {
                	ga360Obj.pageMetaAndDimension();
				}
            }

            if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') ga360Obj.productDetailViews();
		});

        ga360Obj = {
            basketValue: '',
            basketQuantityMainProducts: '',
            basketQuantity: '',
            paymentPayDeliv: '',
            deleteDuplicateProduct: '',
            addToBasket: false,
            removeFromBasket: false,
            changeProduct: false,
            addToBasketLayerOpen: false,
            isSampleOrder: false,
            isBasketVoucher: false,
            prodIndex: '',
            alreadyExists: [[],[]],
            basketValueMainProducts: [],
            opShopCategorisation: [],
            memolist: [],
            basketValueNoMainProducts: [],
            basketValueAllProducts: [],
            relatedProductsSelected: [],
            productOptionsParts: [],
            basketAdditionalOptions: [],
            basketAdditionalOptions2: [],
            basketAdditionalOptions3: [],
            basketAdditionalOptions4: [],
			productNetPrice: [],
            visitedTabs: [],
            wsSessionCookie: '',
            duplicateIndex: '',
            groupProductCategory: {},
			customDimensions: {	},
            samplePrefix: ['CEG', 'VEG', 'VDA'],
            toRemoveFromLS: [
                'productionTime', 'expressDelivery', 'deliveryTime', 'expressProduction', 'deliveryOption', 'designOnline', 'sampleOrder',
                'voucherCodeSet', 'voucherCode', 'allAdditionalOptions', 'allProductConfig', 'numberOfCopies', 'otherProductOptions1',
                'otherProductOptions2', 'otherProductOptions3', 'otherProductOptions4', 'climate', 'groupProductCategory1',
                'groupProductCategory2', 'groupProductCategory3', 'changeIndex', 'changeProduct', 'changeVariantsAll',

            ],
            deliveryIdObj: {
                'ST_PS'     : 'STANDARD',
                'EX_PS'     : 'EXPRESS',
                'EX12_PS'   : 'EXPRESS_12',
                'EX_SZ'     : 'EXPRESS_SA',
                'ST_PS_B2B' : 'STANDARD',
                'ST_PS_BOX' : 'STANDARD',
				'ST-UPS-15' : 'STANDARD',
				'ST-UPS-30' : 'STANDARD',
				'ST-DPD-15' : 'STANDARD',
				'ST-DPD-30' : 'STANDARD',
				'EX18-UPS-15' : 'EXPRESS',
				'EX18-UPS-30' : 'EXPRESS',
				'EX-DPD-15' : 'EXPRESS',
				'EX-DPD-30' : 'EXPRESS',

            addToCart: function() {
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'EECaddToCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': 'EUR',
                        'add': {
                            'products': [{
                                'price': ga360Obj.getProdPrice(),
                                'brand': '',
                                'category': ga360Obj.getProductValues('categoryNameEn'),
                                'variant': ga360Obj.getRelatedProductsSelected().additionalOptions,
                                'id': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions1'),
                                'name': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions1'),
                                'quantity': '1',
                                'numberOfCopies': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions4'),
                                'otherProductOptions1': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions1'),
                                'otherProductOptions2': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions2'),
                                'otherProductOptions3': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions3'),
                                'otherProductOptions4': ga360Obj.getProductValues('otherProductOptions4'),
                                'designOnline': ga360Obj.getDesignOnline(),
                                'expressProduction': ga360Obj.getProductValues('expressProduction'),
                                'sampleOrder': ga360Obj.getProductValues('sampleOrder'),
                                'groupProductCategory1': ga360Obj.getProductValues('groupProductCategory1'),
                                'groupProductCategory2': ga360Obj.getProductValues('groupProductCategory2'),
                                'groupProductCategory3': ga360Obj.getProductValues('groupProductCategory3')
                            }]
                        }
                    }
                });
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket'  || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist') {
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('catIndex');
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('dataCheck');
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('categoryName');
                    ga360Obj.getSetProductionAndDeliveryTime('deliveryTime');
                    ga360Obj.getSetProductionAndDeliveryTime('productionTime');
                }
                ga360Obj.initializeAddToCart();
            },
            addToCartLogic: function() {
                //if(ga360Obj.addToBasket && !ga360Obj.isBasketVoucher)
                if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
					if(opWsVars.templateName != 'ws_memolist') {
						console.log('ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll = ',ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll);
						if(ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll != null) {
							console.log('%cEECaddToCart wird gefeuert 1','color:#098c98');
							ga360Obj.addToCart();
						} else if(ga360Obj.isDuplicate) {
							console.log('%cEECaddToCart wird gefeuert 2','color:#098c98');
							ga360Obj.addToCart();
							ga360Obj.isDuplicate = false;
						}
					} else if (opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist') {
						if(ga360Obj.otherProductOptions1Memo != null) {
							console.log('%cEECaddToCart wird gefeuert 3','color:#098c98');
							ga360Obj.addToCart();
						}
					}
                }
            },
            checkout: function() {
                var ga360LS = localStorage.getItem('ga360'), ga360LSObj, products = [], prObjIndex = '', i = 1;
                if (ga360LS !== null) ga360LSObj = JSON.parse(ga360LS);

                
                for(;i<ws_basket.length;i++) {
                    // Hauptprodukt
                    if(ws_basket[i][9] || (!ws_basket[i][9] && !ws_basket[i][10])) {
                        prObjIndex === '' ? prObjIndex=0 : prObjIndex++;
                        if(!products[prObjIndex]) products[prObjIndex] = {};
                        products[prObjIndex].name = ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].id =  ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].price = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(ws_basket[i][8]);
                        products[prObjIndex].brand = '';
                        products[prObjIndex].category = ga360LSObj.categoryNameEn[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].quantity = '1';
                        products[prObjIndex].numberOfCopies = ws_basket[i][4];
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions1 = ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions2 = ga360Obj.getProductOptions(ws_basket[i][1], ws_basket[i][6], ws_basket[i][4]).option2;
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions3 = ga360Obj.getProductOptions(ws_basket[i][1], ws_basket[i][6], ws_basket[i][4]).option3;
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions4 = ws_basket[i][4];
                        products[prObjIndex].designOnline = op_w2p[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].expressProduction = ga360LSObj.expressProduction[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].sampleOrder = ga360Obj.getSampleOrderPurchase(ws_basket[i][6]);
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory1 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory1[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory2 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory2[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory3 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory3[prObjIndex];
                    // UnterVarianten
                    } else {
                        if(products[prObjIndex] != null) {
                            if(products[prObjIndex].variant == null) products[prObjIndex].variant = [];
                            products[prObjIndex].variant.push(ws_basket[i][1]);
                        }
                    }
                };

                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'EECcheckout',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': 'EUR',
                        'checkout': {
                            'actionField': {
                                'step': ga360Obj.step,
                                'option': 'standard'
                            },
                            'products': products
                        }
                    }
                });
            },
            formatPriceForTracking: function(price) {
                var priceString = price.toString(),
                    formatedPrice = '';

                priceString.indexOf(',') != -1 ? formatedPrice = oplib.modifyPriceForCalculation(priceString) : formatedPrice = parseFloat(priceString);

                formatedPrice = formatedPrice.toFixed(2);
                formatedPrice = formatedPrice.replace('.',',');
                return formatedPrice;
            },
            genericEvents: function(eventCategory, eventAction, eventLabel) {
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'gaEvent',
                    'eventCategory': eventCategory,
                    'eventAction': eventAction,
                    'eventLabel': eventLabel
                });
            },
            getAllVariantsOnProductChange: function() {
                return {
                    'relatedProductsSelected': ga360Obj.getRelatedProductsSelected(),
                    'expressProduction': ga360Obj.getExpressProduction()
                };
            },
            getBasketQuantityNoMainProducts: function() {
                var basketQuantityNoMainProducts;
                basketQuantityNoMainProducts = parseInt(ga360Obj.basketQuantity)-parseInt(ga360Obj.basketQuantityMainProducts);
                basketQuantityNoMainProducts = JSON.stringify(basketQuantityNoMainProducts);
                if (basketQuantityNoMainProducts == 'null') basketQuantityNoMainProducts = '';
                return basketQuantityNoMainProducts;
            },
            getBasketValue: function() {
                var basketValue = ga360Obj.basketValue;
                if(basketValue !== '') {
                    basketValue = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(basketValue);
                }
                return basketValue;
            },
            getBasketValueProducts: function(type, index) {
                var template = opWsVars.templateName, basketValueProducts = [], replacedValue, i = 0, result = 0;
                switch (type) {
                    case 'main':
                        basketValueProducts = ga360Obj.basketValueMainProducts;
                        break;
                    case 'noMain':
                        basketValueProducts = ga360Obj.basketValueNoMainProducts;
                        break;
                    case 'all':
                        basketValueProducts = ga360Obj.basketValueAllProducts[index];
                        break;
                };
                if(basketValueProducts != null) {
                    if(basketValueProducts.length > 0) {
                        for (; i < basketValueProducts.length; i++) {
                            replacedValue = oplib.modifyPriceForCalculation(basketValueProducts[i]);
                            result = result+replacedValue;
                        }
                        result = result.toFixed(2);
                        result = oplib.formatPrice(result);

                        result = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(result);

                    } else {
                        result = '';
                    }
                } else {
                    result = '';
                }
                return result;
            },
            getBrand: function() {
                var brand = '';
                
                    
                        brand = 'onlineprinters';
                    
                
                return brand;
            },
            getCategoryName: function(element) {
                var categoryName = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element),
                    changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct'),
                    changeIndex = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeIndex');
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_category' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' ) {
                    if(element === 'categoryNameEn') categoryName = ga360Obj.opShopCategorisationEn;
                    if(element === 'categoryName') {
                        $.each(ga360Obj.opShopCategorisation, function(index, value) {
                            if(index === 0) categoryName = ga360Obj.opShopCategorisation[index];
                            if(index > 0) categoryName = categoryName+'='+ga360Obj.opShopCategorisation[index];
                        });
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && changeProduct) {
                    categoryName = categoryName[changeIndex];
                }
                return categoryName;
            },
            getBusinessIndustry: function() {
                var businessIndustry = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('businessIndustry'),
                    businessIndustryRaw;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_address_combined' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_address_manage') {
                    businessIndustryRaw = $('.opidTaglistInput').val();
                    if(businessIndustryRaw != null && businessIndustryRaw !== '') {
                        businessIndustry = businessIndustryRaw.split(';');
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'businessIndustry':businessIndustry});
                    }
                }
                return businessIndustry;
            },
            getCatIndex:function() {
                var changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct'),
                    changeIndex = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeIndex'),
                    catIndex;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_category' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    catIndex = '';
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && changeProduct) {
                    catIndex = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('catIndex');
                    catIndex = catIndex[changeIndex];
                }
                return catIndex;
            },
			/*
				Gibt den Bezeichner des geklickten Elements zurÃ¼ck. DafÃ¼r steht an allen relevanten Links ein "el"-Attribut.
				In diesem wird der Bezeichner fÃ¼r das geklickete Element eingetragen.
				SonderfÃ¤lle werden in dieser Funktion behandelt.
			*/
			getClickedElement: function (element) {
				var param_el = element.attr('el');
				switch (param_el) {
					case 'onsiteteaser': param_el += element.parent().index(); break;
				}
				return param_el;
			},
            getClimate: function() {
                var climate = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('climate'),
                    climateNeutral;
                if(climate === '') climate = '0,00';

                // Anzeigen von true & false Anhand der Kosten
                if(climate === '0,00') {
                    climateNeutral = false;
                } else {
                    climateNeutral = true;
                }

                return {'climateNeutral':climateNeutral, 'climate':climate};
            },
            getDataCheck: function() {
                var changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct'),
                    dataCheck = '', selectedElements, changeDataCheck;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    selectedElements = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeVariantsAll');
                    $.each(selectedElements, function(index, value) {
                        if(value.indexOf('DD00') > -1) {
                            dataCheck = false;
                        } else if(value.indexOf('DD01') > -1) {
                            dataCheck = true;
                        }
                    });
                    ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeDataCheck':dataCheck});
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && changeProduct) {
                    changeDataCheck = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeDataCheck');
                    dataCheck = changeDataCheck;
                }
                return dataCheck;
            },
            getDeliveryOnChange: function(deliveryElementId) {
                var subShopId = '15-aa',
                    deliveryId,
                    partSubShopId = subShopId.split('-');
                $.each(ga360Obj.deliveryIdObj, function(key, value) {
                    if(deliveryElementId.indexOf(key) > -1) {
                        if(partSubShopId[0] === 'staging') partSubShopId[0] = '01';
                        deliveryId = partSubShopId[0]+'_'+key;
                        opWsVars.versandart = value;
                        opWsVars.versandid = deliveryId;
                    }
                });
                $(document).trigger('calculateProductionTime', ['versandartchange']);
            },
            getDeliveryOption: function() {
                var deliveryOption = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('deliveryOption');
				
                	if(deliveryOption === '') deliveryOption = 'ST_PS';
				
                return deliveryOption;
            },
            getDesignOnline: function() {
                var designOnline = op_w2p, designOnlineLS, designOnlineDelta;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    if (designOnline === '') {
                        designOnline = 'Ohne Gestaltung';
                    } else {
                        designOnline = op_w2p;
                    }
                    if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        designOnlineLS = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('designOnline');
                        if(designOnlineLS === '') designOnlineLS = [];
						designOnlineLS.push(designOnline);
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'designOnline':designOnlineLS});
                    }
                }
                if(opWsVars.templateName !== 'ws_product' && opWsVars.templateName !== 'ws_basket') designOnline = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('designOnline');
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) designOnline = designOnline[ga360Obj.duplicateIndex];
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && ga360Obj.removeFromBasket) designOnline = designOnline[ga360Obj.deleteIndex];
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist') {
                    designOnline = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('designOnline');
                    if(designOnline === '') designOnline = [];
                    if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        if($.isArray(ga360Obj['designOnlineMemo'])) {
                            designOnlineDelta = ga360Obj['designOnlineMemo'][ga360Obj.memoIndex];
                            designOnlineLS = designOnline;
                            designOnlineLS.push(designOnlineDelta);
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'designOnline':designOnlineLS});
                            designOnline = designOnlineDelta;
                        }
                    }
                }
                return designOnline;
            },
            getElementValue: function(element) {
                switch (element) {
                    case 'otherProductOptions1':
						elementValue = ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll[0];
                        break;
                    case 'otherProductOptions2':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll[1];
                        break;
                    case 'otherProductOptions3':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll[2];
                        break;
                    case 'otherProductOptions4':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.productOptionsParts.variantsAll[3];
                        break;
                    case 'expressProduction':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getExpressProduction();
                        break;
                    case 'sampleOrder':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getSampleOrder();
                        break;
                    case 'groupProductCategory1':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getGroupProductCategory().groupProductCategory1;
                        break;
                    case 'groupProductCategory2':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getGroupProductCategory().groupProductCategory2;
                        break;
                    case 'groupProductCategory3':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getGroupProductCategory().groupProductCategory3;
                        break;
                    case 'catIndex':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getCatIndex();
                        break;
                    case 'dataCheck':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getDataCheck();
                        break;
                    case 'categoryNameEn':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getCategoryName('categoryNameEn');
                        break;
                    case 'categoryName':
                        elementValue = ga360Obj.getCategoryName('categoryName');
                        break;
                }
                return elementValue;
            },
            getEnvironment: function() {
                var environment = '';
                
                
                    
                    
                        environment = 'live';
                    
                
                return environment;
            },
            getExpressDelivery: function() {
                var expressDelivery = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('expressDelivery');
                if(expressDelivery === '') expressDelivery = false;
                return expressDelivery;
            },
            getExpressProduction: function() {
                var expressProduction = '', dataVarindex, dataPrice,
                    changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct');
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    $.each($('select'), function() {
                        dataVarindex = $(this).attr('data-varindex');
                        dataPrice = $(this).attr('data-price');
                        if(dataVarindex != null) {
                            if(dataVarindex.indexOf('YY') > -1) {
                                if(dataPrice === '0,00') {
                                    expressProduction = false;
                                } else {
                                    expressProduction = true;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeExpressProduction':expressProduction});
                } else if(changeProduct) {
                    var changeExpressProduction = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeExpressProduction');
                    expressProduction = changeExpressProduction;
                }
                return expressProduction;
            },
            getGroupProductCategory: function() {
                var groupProductCategory = {'groupProductCategory1':'','groupProductCategory2':'','groupProductCategory3':''},
                    changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct');
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_category') {
                    groupProductCategory = ga360Obj.groupProductCategory;
                    if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeGroupProductCategory':groupProductCategory});
                } else if(changeProduct) {
                    var changeGroupProductCategory = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeGroupProductCategory');
                    groupProductCategory = changeGroupProductCategory;
                }
                return groupProductCategory;
            },
			/*
				Um shopÃ¼bergreifend zu wissen, welche Seite wie gut besucht wird, soll der Pfad der Seite abgespeichert werden.
				Dank der hreflangs wissen wir auch in den meisten FÃ¤llen, welche Seite das im DE-Shop entspricht.
				Somit geben wir den DE-Pfad zurÃ¼ck, wenn existent. Ansonsten den derzeitigen Pfad.
				(Fallback sinnvoll, da 02-aa und 23-aa keine hreflangs existieren)
			*/
			getHreflangPath: function () {
				var hreflangPath = "NULL";
				if ($('link[hreflang="de-DE"]').length) {
					hreflangPath = new URL($('link[hreflang="de-DE"]').attr('href')).pathname;
				}
				else {
					hreflangPath = new URL(document.location.href).pathname;
				}
				return hreflangPath;
			},
            getLocalStorageGa360: function(key) {
                var ga360LS = localStorage.getItem('ga360'), result = '', ga360LSObj;
                if (ga360LS !== null) {
                    ga360LSObj = JSON.parse(ga360LS);
                    result = ga360LSObj[key];
                    if(result == null) result = '';
                }
                return result;
            },
            getProductValues: function(element) {
                var tempObj = {}, elementLS = element+'LS', changeIndex,
                    changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct');
                this[element] = [];
                this[elementLS];
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' || (opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && changeProduct)) {
                    this[element] = ga360Obj.getElementValue(element);
                }
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_category') {
                    switch (element) {
                        case 'catIndex':
                        case 'groupProductCategory1':
                        case 'groupProductCategory2':
                        case 'groupProductCategory3':
                        case 'categoryNameEn':
                        case 'categoryName':
                            this[element] = ga360Obj.getElementValue(element);
                            break;
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        if($.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0]) === -1) {
                            this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                            if(this[elementLS] === '') this[elementLS] = [];
                            this[elementLS].push(this[element]);
                            tempObj[element] = this[elementLS];
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360(tempObj);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0].push(element);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1].push(this[element]);
                        }
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket') {
                    if(!ga360Obj.addToBasket && !ga360Obj.removeFromBasket && !changeProduct) {
                        this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                        this[element] = this[elementLS];
                    } else if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        if($.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0]) === -1) {
                            this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                            this[element] = this[elementLS][ga360Obj.duplicateIndex];
							if(this[elementLS] === '') this[elementLS] = [];
                            this[elementLS].push(this[element]);
                            tempObj[element] = this[elementLS];
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360(tempObj);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0].push(element);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1].push(this[element]);
                        } else {
                            this[element] = ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1][$.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0])];
                        }
                    } else if(ga360Obj.removeFromBasket) {
                        if($.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0]) === -1) {
                            this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                            this[element] = this[elementLS][ga360Obj.deleteIndex];
                            this[elementLS].splice(ga360Obj.deleteIndex,1);
                            tempObj[element] = this[elementLS];
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360(tempObj);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0].push(element);
                            ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1].push(this[element]);
                        } else {
                            this[element] = ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1][$.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0])];
                        }
                    } else if(changeProduct) {
                        changeIndex = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeIndex');
                        this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                        this[elementLS][changeIndex] = this[element];
                        tempObj[element] = this[elementLS];
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360(tempObj);
                        this[element] = this[elementLS];
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist') {
                    if(!ga360Obj.addToBasket && !ga360Obj.removeFromBasket && !changeProduct) {
                        this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                        this[element] = this[elementLS];
                    } else if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        if($.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0]) === -1) {
							var memoName = element+'Memo';
                            if($.isArray(ga360Obj[memoName])) {
                                this[elementLS] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                                if(this[elementLS] === '') this[elementLS] = [];
                                this[element] = ga360Obj[memoName][ga360Obj.memoIndex];
                                this[elementLS].push(this[element]);
                                tempObj[element] = this[elementLS];
                                ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360(tempObj);
                                ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0].push(element);
								ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1].push(this[element]);
							}
                        } else {
                            this[element] = ga360Obj.alreadyExists[1][$.inArray(element,ga360Obj.alreadyExists[0])];
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    this[element] = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(element);
                    if(this[element] === '') this[element] = [];
                }
                return this[element];
            },
            getPageCategory: function() {
                var pageCategory = 'Home',
                    pageType = 'Home',
                    groupProductCategory1 = ga360Obj.groupProductCategory1,
                    templateObj = {
                        'W2P-Category': ['w2p_tpl_category_standard'],      // Hier soll das  uebergeben werden
                        'Configurator': ['w2p_tpl_configurator'],
                        'Error': ['ws_notify']
                    };
                
                if(pageType === 'PDP' || pageType === 'Category') {
                    pageCategory = groupProductCategory1;
                } else if(pageCategory == null || pageCategory === '') {
                    $.each(templateObj,function(key, value) {
                        if($.inArray(opWsVars.templateName, value ) > -1) {
                            pageCategory = key;
                        } else {
                            pageCategory = '';
                        }
                    });
                }
                
                if(pageType === 'PDP' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    pageCategory = 'Add Basket';
                }
                return pageCategory;
            },
            getPageType: function() {
                var pageType = 'Home',
                    templateObj = {
                        'Design Online': ['w2p_tpl_category_standard','w2p_tpl_configurator'],
                        'Error': ['ws_notify']
                    };
                if(pageType == null || pageType === '') {
                    $.each(templateObj,function(key, value) {
                        if($.inArray(opWsVars.templateName, value ) > -1) {
                            pageType = key;
                        } else {
                            pageType = '';
                        }
                    });
                
                } else if(pageType === 'PDP' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    pageType = 'Basket';
                }
                return pageType;
            },
            getPaymentType: function() {
                var paymentTypeId = '';
                if(ga360Obj.paymentPayDeliv !== '') {
                    paymentTypeId = ga360Obj.paymentPayDeliv;
                } else {

            },
			 /*
				Gibt die letzte besuchte Seite zurÃ¼ck. 
			*/
			getPreviousSite: function () {
				//fÃ¼ge param ref an url hinzu
				var state = ws_state.toLowerCase();
				switch (state) {
					case 'product': state += '/' + ws_prod_number; break;
					case 'category': state += '/' + ws_cat_index; break;
					case 'template': state += '/' + (ws_template_name || '').replace(/\|/g,'&vert;');break; //ersetze | durch &vert;, damit es die Auswertung nicht kaputt macht
				}
				return state;
			},
            getProdPrice: function() {
                var price = ga360Obj.productNetPrice[0];
				var prNumberPart = ga360Obj.prodIndex.substring(0, 3);

				if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    if($.inArray(prNumberPart, ga360Obj.samplePrefix) > -1) {
						price = ga360Obj.productNetPrice[1];
					}
				}
				
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && ga360Obj.removeFromBasket) {
                    price = ga360Obj.deletePrice;
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    if($.isArray(ga360Obj['priceMemo'])) {
                        price = ga360Obj['priceMemo'][ga360Obj.memoIndex];
                    }
                }

                if(price != null) {
                    price = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(price);
                }

                return price;
            },
            getProductOptions: function(articleNumber, prIndex, quantity) {
                var optionsArr, option2 = '', option3 = '', substring;
                var optionsArr, option2, option3, substring;
                if(articleNumber) {
                    substring = articleNumber.replace(prIndex, '');
                    substring = substring.replace(quantity, '');
                    if(substring.length > 0) {
                        optionsArr = substring.match(/.{1,3}/g);
                        optionsArr[0] != null ? option2 = optionsArr[0] : option2 = '';
                        optionsArr[1] != null ? option3 = optionsArr[1] : option3 = '';
                    } else {
                        option2 = '';
                        option3 = '';
                    }
                }
                return {'option2':option2, 'option3':option3};
            },
            getPromotionData: function(element, event) {
                var promotionClickString = $(element).attr('data-ga360promoclick'),
                    promotionClickObj = {}, promotionCreativeId = '', promotionCampaignName = '', promotionCreativeName = '',
                    index = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSliderWrapper').index(element);
                try {
                    promotionClickObj = JSON.parse(promotionClickString);
                    promotionCreativeId = promotionClickObj.PromotionCreativeID;
                    promotionCampaignName = promotionClickObj.PromotionCampaignName;
                    promotionCreativeName = promotionClickObj.PromotionCreativeName;
                }
                catch(e) {
                    //console.log('%cpromotionClickError: '+e,'color:#098c98');
                };

                if(event === 'click') {
                    // Reihenfolge der Parameter: id, name, creative, position
                    ga360Obj.promotionClick(promotionCreativeId, promotionCampaignName, promotionCreativeName, index);
                } else if(event == 'impression') {
                    // Reihenfolge der Parameter: id, name, creative, position
                    ga360Obj.promotionImpression(promotionCreativeId, promotionCampaignName, promotionCreativeName, index);
                }
            },
            getRelatedProductsSelected: function(articleNumber) {
                var productIdArr = [], additionalOptionsArr = [], productConfig, dataVarIndex, duplicateVariants,
                    duplicateProductConf, prNumberPart, productConfigArr,
                    allAdditionalOptionsArr = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('allAdditionalOptions'),
                    allProductConfigArr = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('allProductConfig'),
                    changeProduct = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeProduct');
                if(allAdditionalOptionsArr === '') allAdditionalOptionsArr = [];
                if(allProductConfigArr === '') allProductConfigArr = [];
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
					if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
						prNumberPart = ga360Obj.prodIndex.substring(0, 3);
                        if($.inArray(prNumberPart, ga360Obj.samplePrefix) === -1) {
                            ga360Obj.isSampleOrder = false;
                            $.each($('.opidProductConfDepVariation, .opidAdditionalOptionsDepVariation'), function() {
                                dataVarIndex = $(this).attr('data-varindex');
                                if(!productIdArr.includes(dataVarIndex)) {
                                    productIdArr.push(dataVarIndex);
                                    ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected.push(dataVarIndex);
                                }
                            });
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeVariantsAll':ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected});
                        } else if($.inArray(prNumberPart, ga360Obj.samplePrefix) > -1) {
                            ga360Obj.isSampleOrder = true;
                            $.each($('.opidSelectDepVariationsSample'), function() {
                                dataVarIndex = $(this).attr('data-varindex');
                                if(!productIdArr.includes(dataVarIndex)) {
                                    productIdArr.push(dataVarIndex);
                                    ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected.push(dataVarIndex);
                                }
                            });
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeVariantsAll':ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected});
                        }
					} else {
						ga360Obj.isSampleOrder = false;
						$.each($('.opidProductConfDepVariation, .opidAdditionalOptionsDepVariation'), function() {
							dataVarIndex = $(this).attr('data-varindex');
							if(!productIdArr.includes(dataVarIndex)) {
								productIdArr.push(dataVarIndex);
								ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected.push(dataVarIndex);
							}
						});
						ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'changeVariantsAll':ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected});
					}
                    additionalOptionsArr = productIdArr;
                    productConfig = productIdArr[0];
                    additionalOptionsArr.shift();
                    allAdditionalOptionsArr.push(additionalOptionsArr);
                    allProductConfigArr.push(productConfig);
                    ga360Obj.setOtherProductOptions(ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected[0]);
                    if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allAdditionalOptions':allAdditionalOptionsArr});
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allProductConfig':allProductConfigArr});
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket') {
                    if(ga360Obj.duplicateIndex !== '') {
                        duplicateVariants = allAdditionalOptionsArr[ga360Obj.duplicateIndex];
                        duplicateProductConf = allProductConfigArr[ga360Obj.duplicateIndex];
                        allAdditionalOptionsArr.push(duplicateVariants);
                        allProductConfigArr.push(duplicateProductConf);
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allAdditionalOptions':allAdditionalOptionsArr});
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allProductConfig':allProductConfigArr});
                        ga360Obj.setOtherProductOptions(duplicateProductConf);
                        if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                            additionalOptionsArr = duplicateVariants;
                        }
                    } else if(changeProduct) {
                    var changeProductConf = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('changeVariantsAll');
                        changeProductConf = changeProductConf[0];
                        ga360Obj.setOtherProductOptions(changeProductConf);
                    }
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    if($.isArray(ga360Obj['variantMemo'])) {
                        additionalOptionsArr = ga360Obj['variantMemo'][ga360Obj.memoIndex];
                        allAdditionalOptionsArr.push(additionalOptionsArr);
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allAdditionalOptions':allAdditionalOptionsArr});
                    }
                    if($.isArray(ga360Obj['allProductConfigMemo'])) {
                        productConfigArr = ga360Obj['allProductConfigMemo'][ga360Obj.memoIndex];
                        allProductConfigArr.push(productConfigArr);
                        ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allProductConfig':allProductConfigArr});
                    }
                }
                ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected = [];
                if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    productIdArr = '';
                }
                return {'productId':productIdArr,'additionalOptions':additionalOptionsArr};
            },
            getRelatedProductsAll: function() {
                var productIdArr = [], dataVarIndex;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product') {
                    $.each($('.opidProductConfDepVariation, .opidAdditionalOptionsDepVariation').find('option'), function() {
                        dataVarIndex = $(this).attr('data-varindex');
                        if(!productIdArr.includes(dataVarIndex)) {
                            productIdArr.push(dataVarIndex);
                        }
                    });
                }
                if(ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    productIdArr = '';
                }
                return productIdArr;
            },

                    sampleOrder;

                    sampleOrder = ga360Obj.isSampleOrder;
                } else if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket' && changeProduct) {

                }

            },

			/*
				Gibt die searchquery der NEOSseiten zurÃ¼ck.

			getSearchQuery: function () {

				if (opWsVars.templateName == "FullWidth") {
					//das replace ist notwendig, damit ein vom Kunden geschriebenes | keine 
					//Auswirkung auf die Struktur des Contents hat
					return param_otp2.replace("|", "&vert;");

				return "NULL";
			},
            getSetProductionAndDeliveryTime: function(lsValue) {
                var resultLS = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360(lsValue);
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_memolist' && ga360Obj.addToBasket) {
                    switch(lsValue) {
                        case 'deliveryTime':
                            resultLS.push(["",""]);
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'deliveryTime':resultLS});
                            break;
                        case 'productionTime':
                            resultLS.push("");
                            ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'productionTime':resultLS});
                            break;
                    }
                }
                return resultLS;
            },
            getSetWsSessionCookie: function() {
                var excludedTemplates = ['ws_address_combined','ws_user_account'],
                    wsSessionCookie = '071bb22a378aa9b8e10ea332a2fa5c31',
                    wsSessionCookieLS = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('sid');
                if(wsSessionCookie !== wsSessionCookieLS) {
                    
                    
                        if(!excludedTemplates.includes(opWsVars.templateName)) {
                            ga360Obj.removeItemLocalStorageGa360(ga360Obj.toRemoveFromLS);
                        }
                    
                    if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_user_account' && ga360Obj.basketQuantityMainProducts === '') {
                        ga360Obj.removeItemLocalStorageGa360(ga360Obj.toRemoveFromLS);
                    }
                    ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'sid':wsSessionCookie});
                }
            },

            },
            getTax: function() {
                var taxLS = '0,00', tax = '0,00',
                    priceTotalNet, priceTotal;

                ws_basket_total_net.indexOf(',') != -1 ? priceTotalNet = oplib.modifyPriceForCalculation(ws_basket_total_net) : priceTotalNet = parseFloat(ws_basket_total_net);
                ws_basket_total.indexOf(',') != -1 ? priceTotal = oplib.modifyPriceForCalculation(ws_basket_total) : priceTotal = parseFloat(ws_basket_total);


                
                tax = priceTotal - priceTotalNet;
                tax = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(tax);

                return {'tax':tax,'taxLS':taxLS};
            },

            getVoucherCode: function() {
                var voucherCode, voucherNumber, wsDP1;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket') {
                    voucherNumber = $('.opidBasketVoucherInput').val();
                    if(voucherNumber == null || voucherNumber === '') voucherNumber = '';
                    if(voucherNumber == null || voucherNumber === '') {
                        wsDP1 = '';
                        if(wsDP1.indexOf('w2p-') != -1 || wsDP1.indexOf('noOnlineDesign') != -1){
                            voucherNumber = '';
                        }else {
                            voucherNumber = '';
                        }
                    }
                    if(voucherNumber != null && voucherNumber !== '') ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'voucherCode':voucherNumber});
                    voucherCode = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('voucherCode');
                } else {
                    voucherCode = '';
                    if(voucherCode === '') voucherCode = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('voucherCode');
                }
                return voucherCode;
            },
            getVoucherCodeSet: function() {
                var voucherCodeSet = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('voucherCodeSet');
                if(voucherCodeSet === '') voucherCodeSet = false;
                return voucherCodeSet;
            },
            getVoucherCodeConfirm: function(index) {
                var basketComplete = ga360Obj.voucherNumberBasketComplConfirm, categoryObj = ga360Obj.voucherNumberCatIndexConfirm,
                    prodIndexObj = ga360Obj.voucherNumberProdIndexConfirm, prodNumberObj = ga360Obj.voucherNumberProdNumberConfirm,
                    catIndex, prodIndex, prodNumber, result = {'product':'','basketComplete':''};
                if(categoryObj == null) categoryObj = '';
                if(prodIndexObj == null) prodIndexObj = '';
                if(prodNumberObj == null) prodNumberObj = '';
                if(index !== 'false') {
                    catIndex = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('catIndex');
                    catIndex = catIndex[index];
                    prodIndex = ga360Obj.prodIndexConfirm[index];
                    prodNumber = ga360Obj.prodNumberConfirm[index];
                }
                if(categoryObj != null || categoryObj !== '') {
                    if(catIndex === categoryObj.catIndex && categoryObj.voucherNumber != null) {
                        result = {};
                        result.product = categoryObj.voucherNumber;
                        result.basketComplete = '';
                    }
                }
                if(prodIndexObj != null) {
                    if(prodIndex === prodIndexObj.prodIndex && prodIndexObj.voucherNumber != null) {
                        result = {};
                        result.product = prodIndexObj.voucherNumber;
                        result.basketComplete = '';
                    }
                }
                if(prodNumberObj != null) {
                    if(prodNumber === prodNumberObj.prNumber && prodNumberObj.voucherNumber != null) {
                        result = {};
                        result.product = prodNumberObj.voucherNumber;
                        result.basketComplete = '';
                    }
                }
                if(basketComplete != null) {
                    if(basketComplete.basketComplete && basketComplete.voucherNumber != null) {
                        result = {};
                        result.product = '';
                        result.basketComplete = basketComplete.voucherNumber;
                    }
                }
                return result;
            },
			initCustomDimensions: function(){
				oplib.wsSessionStorage.init();
				ga360Obj.initializeTabNumber();

				var previousSite = oplib.wsSessionStorage.getItem('previousSite') || "NULL";
				var clickedElement = oplib.wsSessionStorage.getItem('clickedElement') || "NULL";
				var tabnr = sessionStorage.getItem('tabnr') || "NULL";
				var searchquery = ga360Obj.getSearchQuery() || "NULL";
				var hreflangPath = ga360Obj.getHreflangPath() || "NULL";

				ga360Obj.customDimensions = {
					customDimension: previousSite+'|'+clickedElement+'|'+tabnr+'|'+searchquery+'|'+hreflangPath,
					previousSite: previousSite,
					clickedElement: clickedElement,
					tabnr: tabnr,
					searchquery: searchquery,
					hreflangPath: hreflangPath
				}

				/*
					Nachdem die previousSite und das clickedElement fÃ¼rs Tracking gesetzt sind, werden diese zurÃ¼ckgesetzt.
					Notwendig, um folgenden Fall zu verhindern (Beispiel mit PreviousSite, gilt auch fÃ¼r clickedElement):
					1. Seite1 setzt previousSite
					2. Seite2 trackt previousSite, setzt diese aber nicht neu
					3. Seite3 trackt previousSite von Seite 1, da diese nicht geÃ¤ndert wurde
				*/
				oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('previousSite', 'NULL');
				oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('clickedElement', 'NULL');
			},
            initializeAddToCart: function() {
                ga360Obj.addToBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.duplicateIndex = '';
                ga360Obj.changeProduct = false;
                ga360Obj.alreadyExists = [[],[]];
                ga360Obj.isSampleOrder = false;
            },
            initializePageMetaAndDimension: function() {
                ga360Obj.addToBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.removeFromBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.changeProduct = false;
                if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket') {
                    ga360Obj.removeItemLocalStorageGa360(['changeProduct','changeIndex','changeVariantsAll']);
                }
            },
            initializeProductDetailViews: function() {
                ga360Obj.addToBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.removeFromBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.changeProduct = false;
            },
            initializeRemoveFromCartAfter: function() {
                ga360Obj.removeFromBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.changeProduct = false;
                ga360Obj.alreadyExists = [[],[]];
            },
            initializeRemoveFromCartBefore: function() {
                ga360Obj.addToBasket = false;
                ga360Obj.changeProduct = false;
            },
			/*  
				Diese Funktion initialisiert die Tabnummer im Sessionstorage
				Ein Tab hat (meist) einen eigenen, unabhÃ¤ngigen Sessionstorage. Nur wenn ein Tab geklont wird (Rechtsklick, "Tab klonen"), 
				ist dies nicht der Fall. 
				Somit kÃ¶nnen wir in mehr als 90% aller Multi-Tab-User die Tabnummer mit angeben.

				DafÃ¼r wird im LocalStorage, besser gesagt im wsSessionStorage gezÃ¤hlt, wie viele Tabs initialisiert wurden. 
				Im WsSessionStorage befindet sich die hÃ¶chste Anzahl an Tabs, die in dieser Websale-Session aufgemacht wurde.
			*/
			initializeTabNumber: function () {
				/*
					Ein Tab hat bereits eine Nummer? Gib diese zurÃ¼ck.
				*/
				var tabNumber = sessionStorage.getItem('tabnr');

				if (tabNumber != undefined) {
					return;
				}

				/*
					Existiert ein Eintrag im localStorage? Dann erhÃ¶he den Counter mit 1. Ansonsten trage als Tabnummer 1 ein.
				*/
				var maxTabNumber = oplib.wsSessionStorage.getItem('maxTabNumber') || 0;

				maxTabNumber += 1;
				oplib.wsSessionStorage.setItem('maxTabNumber', maxTabNumber);
				sessionStorage.setItem('tabnr', maxTabNumber);

				return maxTabNumber;
			},
			internalSearch: function(keyword) {
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'Internal Search',
                    'keyword': keyword
                });
            },
            isInViewport: function(element) {
                var elementTop = $(element).offset().top,
                    elementBottom = elementTop + $(element).outerHeight(),
                    viewportTop = $(window).scrollTop(),
                    viewportTopInclHeader = viewportTop + 37,
                    isInViewport,
                    viewportBottom = viewportTop + $(window).height();
                elementBottom > viewportTopInclHeader && elementTop < viewportBottom ? isInViewport = true : isInViewport = false;
                return isInViewport;
            },
            isLoggedIn: function() {
                var loggedIn = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('isLoggedIn'),
                    stLoggedIn = false;
                (loggedIn || stLoggedIn) ? loggedIn = true : loggedIn = false;
                return loggedIn;
            },

            productDetailViews: function() {

            removeFromCart: function() {
                ga360Obj.initializeRemoveFromCartBefore();
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'EECremoveFromCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'remove': {
                            'actionField': '',

				if(opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_product' || opWsVars.templateName === 'ws_basket') {
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('catIndex');
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('dataCheck');
                    ga360Obj.getProductValues('categoryName');
                }
                ga360Obj.initializeRemoveFromCartAfter();
            },
            removeFromCartVariantsAndProductsConfig: function() {
                var removeFromCartVariants = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('allAdditionalOptions'),
                    removeFromCartProductConfig = ga360Obj.getLocalStorageGa360('allProductConfig'),
                    allAdditionalOptionsDelta,
                    allProductConfigDelta;
                allAdditionalOptionsDelta = removeFromCartVariants;
                allProductConfigDelta = removeFromCartProductConfig;
                removeFromCartVariants = allAdditionalOptionsDelta[ga360Obj.deleteIndex];
                removeFromCartProductConfig = allProductConfigDelta[ga360Obj.deleteIndex];
                allAdditionalOptionsDelta.splice(ga360Obj.deleteIndex,1);
                allProductConfigDelta.splice(ga360Obj.deleteIndex,1);
                ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allAdditionalOptions':allAdditionalOptionsDelta});
                ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'allProductConfig':allProductConfigDelta});
                return {'removeFromCartVariants':removeFromCartVariants,'removeFromCartProductsConfig':removeFromCartProductConfig};
            },
            removeItemLocalStorageGa360: function(array) {
                var ga360LS = localStorage.getItem('ga360'), ga360LSObj;
                if (ga360LS !== null) {
                    ga360LSObj = JSON.parse(ga360LS);
                    $.each(array, function(index, value) {
                        delete ga360LSObj[value];
                    });
                    ga360LSString = JSON.stringify(ga360LSObj);
                    localStorage.setItem('ga360', ga360LSString);
                }
            },
            setLocalStorageGa360: function(object) {
                var ga360LS = localStorage.getItem('ga360'), ga360LSObj;
                (ga360LS !== null) ? ga360LSObj = JSON.parse(ga360LS) : ga360LSObj = {};
                $.each(object, function(key, value) {
                    ga360LSObj[key] = value;
                    ga360LSString = JSON.stringify(ga360LSObj);
                    localStorage.setItem('ga360', ga360LSString);
                });
            },
            setOtherProductOptions: function(selectedElement) {
                ga360Obj.productOptionsParts = oplib.sliceProductConfPrNumber(selectedElement);
                ga360Obj.relatedProductsSelected = [];
            },
            setTrustedShops: function() {
                var trustedShopCost = '';
                ga360Obj.setLocalStorageGa360({'trustedShopCost':trustedShopCost});
            },
            splitGroupProductCategories: function() {
                var splitGroupArr = ['','',''];
                if(ga360Obj.groupProductCategory.groupProductCategory1 !== '') {
                    splitGroupArr = ga360Obj.groupProductCategory.groupProductCategory1.split('=');
                }
                return splitGroupArr;
            },

            transactions: function() {
                var ga360LS = localStorage.getItem('ga360'), ga360LSObj, products = [], prObjIndex = '', i = 1;
                if (ga360LS !== null) ga360LSObj = JSON.parse(ga360LS);
                $.each(ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions1, function(index) {
                    products[index] = {};
                    products[index].nameLS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions1[index];
                    products[index].idLS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions1[index];
                    products[index].priceLS = ga360Obj.getBasketValueProducts('all', index);
                    products[index].brandLS = '';
                    products[index].categoryLS = ga360LSObj.categoryNameEn[index];
                    products[index].variantLS = ga360LSObj.allAdditionalOptions[index];
                    products[index].quantityLS = '1';
                    products[index].couponLS = ga360Obj.getVoucherCodeConfirm(index).product;
                    products[index].numberOfCopiesLS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions4[index];
                    products[index].otherProductOptions1LS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions1[index];
                    products[index].otherProductOptions2LS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions2[index];
                    products[index].otherProductOptions3LS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions3[index];
                    products[index].otherProductOptions4LS = ga360LSObj.otherProductOptions4[index];
                    products[index].designOnlineLS = op_w2p[index];
                    products[index].expressProductionLS = ga360LSObj.expressProduction[index];
                    products[index].sampleOrderLS = ga360LSObj.sampleOrder[index];
                    products[index].groupProductCategory1LS = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory1[index];
                    products[index].groupProductCategory2LS = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory2[index];
                    products[index].groupProductCategory3LS = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory3[index];
                });

                
                for(;i<ws_basket.length;i++) {
                    // Hauptprodukt
                    if(ws_basket[i][9] || (!ws_basket[i][9] && !ws_basket[i][10])) {
                        prObjIndex === '' ? prObjIndex=0 : prObjIndex++;
                        if(!products[prObjIndex]) products[prObjIndex] = {};
                        products[prObjIndex].name = ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].id =  ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].price = ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(ws_basket[i][8]);
                        products[prObjIndex].brand = '';
                        products[prObjIndex].category = ga360LSObj.categoryNameEn[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].quantity = '1';
                        products[prObjIndex].coupon = ga360Obj.getVoucherCodeConfirm(prObjIndex).product;
                        products[prObjIndex].numberOfCopies = ws_basket[i][4];
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions1 = ws_basket[i][6];
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions2 = ga360Obj.getProductOptions(ws_basket[i][1], ws_basket[i][6], ws_basket[i][4]).option2;
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions3 = ga360Obj.getProductOptions(ws_basket[i][1], ws_basket[i][6], ws_basket[i][4]).option3;
                        products[prObjIndex].otherProductOptions4 = ws_basket[i][4];
                        products[prObjIndex].designOnline = op_w2p[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].expressProduction = ga360LSObj.expressProduction[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].sampleOrder = ga360Obj.getSampleOrderPurchase(ws_basket[i][6]);
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory1 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory1[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory2 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory2[prObjIndex];
                        products[prObjIndex].groupProductCategory3 = ga360LSObj.groupProductCategory3[prObjIndex];
                    // UnterVarianten
                    } else {
                        if(products[prObjIndex] != null) {
                            if(products[prObjIndex].variant == null) products[prObjIndex].variant = [];
                            products[prObjIndex].variant.push(ws_basket[i][1]);
                        }
                    }
                };

                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'EECpurchase',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': 'EUR',
                        'purchase': {
                            'actionField': {
                                'id': '',
                                'affiliation': 'onlineshop',
                                'revenueLS': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking("0.00"),
                                'revenue': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(ws_basket_total),
                                'taxLS': ga360Obj.getTax().taxLS,
                                'tax': ga360Obj.getTax().tax,
                                'shippingLS': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking("0.00"),
                                'shipping': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(ws_delivery_cost),
                                'coupon': ga360Obj.getVoucherCodeConfirm('false').basketComplete,
                                'climateLS': ga360Obj.getClimate().climate,
                                'climate': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(0,00),
                                'trustedShopCost': ga360Obj.getTrustedShops().trustedShopCost,
                                'discount': ga360Obj.formatPriceForTracking(ws_total_discount)
                            },
                            'products': products
                        }
                    }
                });
            },

            triggerCheckOut: function() {
                var templateNames = ['ws_address_combined', 'ws_pay_deliv', 'ws_place_order', 'ws_confirm'],
                    step = parseInt($.inArray(opWsVars.templateName, templateNames))+1;
                
                if(step > 0) {
                    ga360Obj.step = step;
                    ga360Obj.checkout();
                }
            },
            virtualPageView: function(pagePath) {
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'Page View',
                    'pagePath': pagePath
                });
            }
        };
    </script>

<script>
        (function() {
            $(window).on('load',function() {
                var SLIDE_ANIMATION_DURATION = 600;
                var SLIDE_CHANGE             = 5000;

                var slider;
                var flexSlider = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSlider  .opidMarketingTeaserWrapper').flexslider({
                    namespace:		'marketingTeaserWrapper-',
                    selector:		'.marketingTeaserWrapperSlides > li',
                    manualControls:	'.opidMarketingTeaserSlider .opidMarketingTeaserWrapperControls > li',
                    animation:		'slide',
                    directionNav:	false,
                    pauseOnHover:	false,
                    animationSpeed:	SLIDE_ANIMATION_DURATION,
                    slideshowSpeed:	SLIDE_CHANGE,
                    animationLoop:	true,
                    start: function(e) {
                        
                        
                            var element =  $(e).find('.marketingTeaserWrapper-active-slide'),
                                index = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSliderWrapper').index(element);
                            if(ga360Obj.isInViewport(element)) {
                                if($.inArray(index, ga360Obj.visitedTabs) === -1) {
                                    ga360Obj.visitedTabs.push(index);
                                    ga360Obj.getPromotionData(element, 'impression');
                                }
                            }
                        

                        slider = e;
                    },
                    after: function(e) {
                        
                        
                            var element =  $(e).find('.marketingTeaserWrapper-active-slide'),
                                index = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSliderWrapper').index(element);
                            if(ga360Obj.isInViewport(element)) {
                                if($.inArray(index, ga360Obj.visitedTabs) === -1) {
                                    ga360Obj.visitedTabs.push(index);
                                    ga360Obj.getPromotionData(element, 'impression');
                                }
                            }
                        
                    }
                });

                $('.opidMarketingTeaserSlider').hover(function() {
                    $(this).addClass('s-hover');
                    if (slider) slider.pause();
                }, function() {
                    $(this).removeClass('s-hover');
                    if (slider) slider.play();
                });

                var shineAni = false;

                $('.opidMarketingTeaserSlider button').hover(function() {
                    if (shineAni) return;
                    var shine = $(this).find('.shine');
                    var width = $(this).width() + shine.width();
                    shineAni = true;

                    shine.css('left', '-' + shine.width() + 'px');
                    shine.stop().animate({
                        left: "+="+ width,
                    }, 1000, function()
                    {
                        shineAni = false;
                    });
                });
            });
        })();
    </script>

		function PriceReqMod() {
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('&amp;','&').replace('%28','(').replace('%2d','-').replace('%29',')');
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('http','https');
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('httpss','https');
		}
<!-- ende Incl-PriceRequestURL -->

				});
		</script>

<h2 class="img_startseite_headline"><span class="campaign_markup">//</span>Unsere Neuigkeiten auf einen Blick</h2>
<div class="clear"></div>
<div class="news_box_gesamt" name="StartInfoboxen">
<div class="neos-contentcollection">
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newslist" id="node-57f51bcd3cdca">
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-ftae9oru5x2ka">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/unser-qualitaetstriple"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/d53a89a91fe2b877f614571b76eade2ca3c0ba9d/News_mini_DE.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/unser-qualitaetstriple">Europaweit einzigartig: Unser Qualitäts-Triple</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Als erste Onlinedruckerei Europas hat Onlineprinters nach den Qualitätssiegeln für die Bereiche Offset und Weiterverarbeitung nun auch die Zertifizierung für den Digitaldruck erlangt. 
		<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/unser-qualitaetstriple">[mehr]</a>
</p>
</div>
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-2kd2b018f9r73">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/bester-online-haendler-handelsblatt-studie"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/2f2c554c27b812565e8ddd74a59655446195c80d/Bild_Newskasten_Auszeichnung_Handelsblatt_2019-226x80.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/bester-online-haendler-handelsblatt-studie">Erster Platz bei der Handelsblatt Studie „Bester Online-Händler“</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Zum vierten Mal in Folge landete Onlineprinters bei der Handelsblatt Umfrage „Bester Online-Händler“ erneut auf dem ersten Platz. Die Auszeichnung hat das Unternehmen unter anderem seinem guten Kundenservice zu verdanken. 
		<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/bester-online-haendler-handelsblatt-studie">[mehr]</a>
</p>
</div>
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-nrf4k17zqrdj1">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/Neu-Budget-Kataloge-guenstig-produziert-1"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/ea173ef1d63bbc658eae3017ed16936b8fde6ed4/Budget.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/Neu-Budget-Kataloge-guenstig-produziert-1">Neu: Budget-Kataloge günstig produziert</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Neben unseren klassischen Katalogen für den Druck vieler Bilder bieten wir nun auch eine kostengünstige Alternative für textlastige Druckprodukte wie Bedienungsanleitungen an: Budget-Kataloge. 
		<a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/Neu-Budget-Kataloge-guenstig-produziert-1">[mehr]</a>
</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<div class="box_pr_descr" id="archiv_link">
<span>Alle Neuigkeiten unserer Online-Druckerei finden Sie im</span> <a href="https://de.onlineprinters.ch/c/ueber-uns/aktuelles/archiv" style="">News-Archiv</a>.
				</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="clear"></div>

<script type="text/javascript">
    
    
        $(window).on('resize scroll', function() {
            $(window).on('load',function() {   
             var element =  $('.marketingTeaserWrapper-active-slide'),
                 index = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSliderWrapper').index(element);
           
                if(ga360Obj.isInViewport(element)) {
                    if($.inArray(index, ga360Obj.visitedTabs) === -1) {
                        ga360Obj.visitedTabs.push(index);
                        ga360Obj.getPromotionData(element, 'impression');
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    
</script>
